Operator requirements: LAN dashboard http://10.0.0.71:8090/pipeline.html kept current (5-min render timer + agent status block); every agent heartbeats ≤ 5 min; controller checks agents every 5 min and re-drives stale/off-task ones. Rails: rig steps as launch_rig_unit.sh units (snapshotted scripts), keeper hold for rig work, arm-by-effect (0x158/0x114/0x118/0x10C write-only), one re-run per wedge, positive controls before nulls, flashes only under the full rails, routed WNS ≥ 0, labels [silicon]/[sim]/[netlist]/[inferred], commit -s + trailer, results to two_jup/RXFIX_STATE.md.
Tasks: 1 = T0a harness truth taps + re-score (COMB32_SRO_SIM_TAPS.md); 2 = T0b docs corrections + surveys + SRO sign cross-check; 3 = T0c rig scripts DRY (slackleg_go.sh, witness_read.sh); 4 = T0d dashboard 5-min timer + agent status block + stall 5-min; 5 = T1 slack legs on the rig (enSlack OFF/ON); 6 = T2 RTL fix variant + injector + sim gate; 7 = T3 build + flash 148 + judges (+146); 8 = T4 write-up + final review. 9 = W1 silicon ring-witness instrument (build, no flash); 10 = W1 flash 148 under rails + witness leg; 11 = R3S skip-only steering sim gate; 12 = R4 pre-fill + lock-armed steering sim gate; 12b = R4B silicon-ready steering sim gate; 13 = W1+R4B build + flash 148 + judges; 14 = R4C/R4D FULL-side sim gate (refuted); NIGHT 2: 15 = prereg+preflight; 16 = reverse BEFORE x2; 17 = forward replicate + RSSI; 18 = cross-lineage W1 flash on 146 + controls; 19 = reverse witness leg (branch W/a/b/c); 20 = S1 R4D+R1 sim gate; 21 = S2 R4E push-drop sim gate; 22 = 146 fix build + flash + judges; 23 = forward residual class (desk); 24 = slack replicates/soak; 25 = hand-back + morning report; 26 = host delivery-path resync (forward 0.22 % residual); 27 = deploy resync daemon to 148 + judge + A/B; 28 = displacement-phase re-measurement (desk).
## Facts binding all tasks
- Rate_Handle ring IS guarded (Validate_Input_Push_Pop_block.v:119-137; occupancy MATLAB_Function_block2.v:86-136; empty=0, full=32): empty edge = skipped valid slot (no data loss), full edge = symbol deletion. Never re-centred (reset_1 tied to 0, Rate_Handle.v:116,125; reset_1 is flush-to-empty — never pulse it).
- Harness defect: sim_sro.cpp:115 occupancy = (pushPtr−popPtr)&31 cannot tell 0 from 32; gen_sro_stim.py:72-73: negative ppm drains the ring toward EMPTY.
- Preamble_Detector.Delay10_reg[49331:0] (Preamble_Detector.v:321-334) hard-codes the 1-in-4 cadence as the pop of the 12,333-deep realignment FIFO (full threshold 12333); runtime slack = fixctl bit 3 at 0x208 (Validate_Input_Push_Pop.v:136, FixCtlDec.v:42, addr_decoder.v:869), default OFF; witness counters FIFO.v:214-253 (0x20C/0x210 ownership on the flashed image must be confirmed first — dbgcap re-purposed them 08-30).
- Downstream epoch counters count valids (Peak_Search.v:94, Timing_Adjust.v:126, End_Generator.v:71); 13-slot inter-frame guard (sample_discard_controller inactive) exists.
- Injector rails: 3 loose mirrors + both TxRxCompo_ip_v1_0.zip members (txfix_inject.py:36-41,250-256,305-308,337-361); routed WNS margin +0.11 ns (148) / +0.17 (146).

Task 6: TRACE RESULT [sim] — the death path is NOT the tick-vs-valid divergence. t_m10 _ep.txt: Peak_Search's timingOffset takes exactly TWO values, 30 and 62 (delta = 32 symbols). Steady state 30; at each pop_on_empty frame (f=40,48,56,...) the epoch's argmax latches 62 instead, Timing_Adjust fires SyncPulse at taRef=63 (32 symbols late), and one to two frames later it latches 30 again — the +32/-31-symbol frame-start jumps seen in the demod marks. The frames at the two transitions are the lost frames. D (nCorr-nPop = PD FIFO occupancy) is 12321 steady and dips to 12320 for exactly one epoch after the hole: the tick-vs-valid divergence is REAL, is exactly 1 symbol, and is NOT what kills frames — which is why R1 (which removes it) changes nothing. The competing correlation peak 32 symbols from the true one is the defect; the SRO drift only decides which of the two wins.
Task 6: TRACE accepted. Ruling: R2 = Peak_Search/Timing_Adjust flywheel (±2-symbol tracking window, 2-epoch persistence for jumps, reject witness); gate 0/−2.5/−10/+2.5 + acquisition/re-lock controls — cost if wrong: one more sim round.

Task 6: BISECTION RESULT — origin of the +32 is INCONCLUSIVE, with hard exclusions [sim]. (A) The symbol stream is UNIFORM: per 49,332-sample local frame every stage (Rate_Handle.validOut, CFC, Carrier_Sync, Preamble_Detector, Correlator.validOut) delivers 12,333 symbols on 409 of 417 frames and 12,332 on the 8 pop_on_empty frames; 12,365 never occurs anywhere and the surplus count is 0, so NO symbols are inserted at any stage. (B) Yet the correlator's threshold crossing advances 12,365 valids (+32) into the excursion and 49,460 samples (+128). A and B together mean the winning crossing during the excursion is a DIFFERENT correlation event 32 symbols from the true preamble, not the same peak arriving late — this RETRACTS the "same peak displaced" reading I filed earlier, which rested on the crossing magnitude being smooth; the census evidence is stronger and wins. Combined with the corr dump's one-crossing-per-epoch, it means the TRUE peak drops BELOW threshold during the excursion. (C) But the frames extracted at the +32 offset decode CORRECTLY (golden hash) while a 32-symbol misplacement of the 12,320-symbol window should have destroyed them. Excluded by direct measurement: the Rate_Handle ring, symbol insertion anywhere in the chain, the PD realignment FIFO guards. Remaining, in attack order: (i) the Correlator moving-energy threshold path (Correlator.v:110-177 + ThresholdLimiter) — the only block that can make the true peak vanish; (ii) interpolator strobe placement (Interpolation_Control.v:138-158); (iii) that the loopback's REPEATING BIST payload may make a 32-symbol-shifted extraction hash identical, which would make (C) an artefact of this stimulus — testable with a non-repeating payload. I did not cut R3 blind on this.

Task 6: THRESHOLD PATH — DECISIVE [sim] (d_m10_corr.txt: correlator magnitude and threshold at EVERY beat with tref in [0,90], frames 0..43 of the -10 ppm leg). Through the excursion the THRESHOLD IS FLAT (207.9M .. 208.9M at both positions, every frame) and the PEAK ITSELF MOVES:
  frame | 4*corr@tref=30 |  thr  | 4*corr@tref=62 |  argmax
   38   |  277,556,084   | 208.4M|    79,352,948  |   30
   39   |    1,768,964   | 207.9M|   277,691,012  |   62
   40   |    1,764,160   | 207.9M|   277,822,324  |   62
   41   |  277,222,500   | 207.9M|    79,459,972  |   30
So this is NOT a competitor rising past a drifting threshold and NOT a threshold artefact: the correlation magnitude at the true position COLLAPSES by a factor of 157 (277.6M -> 1.77M) and the identical magnitude APPEARS at +32 symbols. The whole correlation pattern is displaced by exactly 32 symbols for the length of the excursion. (Note also that tref=62 carries a real sub-threshold sidelobe at ~79M = 29 % of the peak in every normal frame.) Combined with (A) no symbols inserted at any stage and the ring exonerated per-beat, the receiver's SAMPLING PHASE must jump by 32 symbols = 128 input samples. That is the coordinator's hypothesis (b): the Rice modulo-1 interpolator counter (Interpolation_Control.v:138-158, countReg mod 1024 at 256 = quarter-sample steps, so 512 steps = 128 samples = 32 symbols) moving by half its range. LOCALISED BY EXCLUSION AND MAGNITUDE, NOT YET PROVEN by a captured countReg jump -- the ring dump's window did not contain one. R3 is NOT cut on that residual doubt; the next task should dump countReg/counter/Delta/mu across the frame boundary at the START of an excursion (not around the pop_on_empty, which sits mid-excursion).

Task 6: NCO / BASEPOINT HYPOTHESIS REFUTED [sim] (e_m10_ring.txt, window mode: one row per interpolator strobe over input samples 1,825,284..2,075,282 = frames 37.00..42.07 of the -10 ppm leg, which brackets the ENTIRE excursion at frames 39-40). 62,500 strobes, i.e. 12,332.6 per frame, uniform. Consecutive strobe spacings are ONLY 3, 4 or 5 input samples (4 on 62,353 of them; the 3s and 5s are the ordinary fractional-timing dither) -- there is NO 128-sample jump, no burst of missing or extra strobes, and no half-field jump of countReg anywhere across the excursion. So the interpolator's basepoint does NOT move by 128 samples and Interpolation_Control.v:138-158 is EXCLUDED as the source, as is the T8.4 clamp interaction. R3-at-the-NCO must not be cut.
Task 6: STATE OF THE HUNT after task 6 — the +32-symbol displacement of the correlation pattern is measured beyond doubt (threshold flat, magnitude at the true position collapses 277.6M -> 1.77M, identical magnitude appears at +32), but EVERY structural mechanism proposed so far is now excluded by direct measurement: the Rate_Handle ring (per-beat pointers/occupancy), symbol insertion or deletion anywhere in the valid chain (uniform 12,333 per local frame at all five stages), the Preamble_Detector realignment FIFO guards (pdPof = 0), the correlator threshold path (flat), and the interpolator strobe/basepoint (uniform 3/4/5-sample spacing across the excursion). What has NOT been examined: the correlator's own datapath -- the 13-tap matched filter and its coefficient/tap alignment (Discrete_FIR_Filter -> Filter/FilterCoef, Correlator.v:68-110), and the possibility that what moves is the CONTENT of the symbols (the interpolated values) rather than their timing, e.g. a phase/AGC/CFO transient that makes the matched filter peak elsewhere. Task 6: DATA-DISPLACEMENT PROBE [sim] (h_m10_ring.txt: one row per Rate_Handle validOut over frames 37.00-42.07 of the -10 ppm leg, 62,499 output symbols; CFO-invariant differential symbols, mean removed and normalised).
  - CONTENT TRANSIENT: NOT indicated. Per-frame mean |y| is 16735 / 16526 alternating and mean arg is -1.03 / -1.00 rad, essentially unchanged through the excursion frames 39-40.
  - ALIGNMENT: consecutive-frame cross-correlation has TWO peaks of nearly equal height, rho = 0.50 at lag 0/-1 and rho = 0.50 at lag +/-32, with every other lag at rho = 0.02. Crucially this double peak is present in EVERY frame pair examined, including 38->39 which is NOT a transition -- so a 32-symbol alignment ambiguity is present in the receiver's symbol stream at ALL times, and the SRO only decides which of the two the correlator's argmax picks in a given epoch. This is consistent with the threshold-path dump (the magnitude simply appears at the other alignment) but it does NOT by itself explain what makes the choice flip.
  - CAVEAT, recorded rather than hidden: the TX frame's own differential autocorrelation shows ~0.18 at EVERY integer-symbol lag (2,4,8,16,32,64,96,128,160,256 samples) and 0.004 at non-integer-symbol lags, i.e. no lag-32 preference. That measurement (within-frame, 4 sps) and the frame-to-frame one (symbol rate) are not directly comparable and I did NOT reconcile them. An earlier version of this cross-correlation omitted the mean removal and produced a flat surface; its lag readings were withdrawn before anything was concluded from them.
Task 6: STOPPING THE HUNT HERE. The displacement is measured beyond doubt and six candidate mechanisms are excluded by direct measurement (ring, symbol insertion/deletion, PD FIFO guards, tick-vs-valid divergence, correlator threshold path, interpolator strobe/basepoint). The remaining lead is the 32-symbol alignment ambiguity that is present in the symbol stream at all times. No R3 was cut: cutting one now would be guessing, and both fixes that were cut on earlier guesses are gated as null and as harmful respectively. Recorded as measured; NOT used to name a stage or to cut R3.

Task 7: THE "32-SYMBOL AMBIGUITY PRESENT AT ALL TIMES" IS REFUTED, AND THE +32 IS NOW LOCALISED TO THE POP_ON_EMPTY BEAT ITSELF [sim]. Re-analysing task 6's own h_m10_ring.txt (no new sim needed): (a) SEGMENTATION IS NOT THE ARTEFACT — segmenting the Rate_Handle validOut symbols by VALID COUNT (12333/frame) and by INPUT SAMPLE (sidx//49332) give the same answer (t7_segcheck.py). (b) The rho(0)=0.50 / rho(+-32)=0.50 "double peak" is a WHOLE-FRAME AVERAGE OF A SHARP LOCALISED STEP, not an ambiguity: quarter-by-quarter, consecutive frames correlate at rho=1.00 at lag 0 for the first half and rho=0.94-0.99 at lag +32 (or -32) for the second half, with the losing lag at 0.00-0.05. Exactly one of +32/-32 is ever active, and its sign flips with the excursion. (c) The step is ONE BEAT WIDE and its position was measured per-symbol: for the -10 ppm window, frames 39->40 switch between symbol i=6013 (sidx 1997334) and i=6014 (sidx 1997342), and a full lag scan over -64..+64 gives mean |b[i]-a[i+L]| = 482 at L=+32 against 18,465 at the next-best lag after the step, and 364 at L=0 against 18,379 next-best before it. (d) THE SWITCH BEAT IS THE POP_ON_EMPTY BEAT: h_m10_ring.txt row sidx=1997338 (between the two) carries strobe=1, pop=1, vout=0, occ=0, popEmpty=1, tref=5976 -- the Rate_Handle EMPTY-edge event. So the +32 is not "present at all times" and does not need a separate mechanism: it is a step of exactly ONE RING LAP (the Rate_Handle FIFO_block ring is 32 entries deep, mod-32 pointers) taken at the instant the guard suppresses a pop. Task 6's exclusion of the ring rested on the per-beat POINTER sequence being clean; a clean pointer sequence does not exclude a lap-distance READ. The ring is back in scope as the source. Tools: t7_segcheck.py (committed).

Task 7: RTL READ PATH NAMED [netlist] (controller item 2). Rate_Handle's ring is FIFO_block.v:184-196 `SimpleDualPortRAM_generic #(.AddrWidth(5), .DataWidth(16))` -- 32 entries, exactly the 32 that is measured. wr_addr = Push_Counter_out1 (:191), wr_en = Validate_Input_Push_Pop_valid_push (:192), rd_addr = Pop_Counter_out1 (:193). Both pointers advance ONLY on the VALIDATED events: FIFO_block.v:127-129 `count_1 = valid_push ? count_value : Push_Counter_out1` and :161-163 `count_4 = valid_pop ? count_value_1 : Pop_Counter_out1`. So a suppressed pop does not move the read pointer -- which is why task 6's per-beat pointer census looked clean and why the ring was (wrongly) excluded on it. The read itself is where the lap lives: SimpleDualPortRAM_generic.v:57-66 registers the read (`data_int <= ram[rd_addr]`) inside the same `always @(posedge clk)` that performs `ram[wr_addr] <= {wr_din_re, wr_din_im}` with NONBLOCKING assignment, i.e. READ-BEFORE-WRITE on a coincident address; and FIFO_block.v:198-202 emits `out_re/out_im = data_int` (one enb tick behind the pointer) while `validPop = valid_pop` is COMBINATIONAL (not delayed). At the EMPTY edge Pop_Counter == Push_Counter, so the coincident push and the suppressed pop hit the SAME address, and the registered read latches the word from the PREVIOUS LAP of the 32-entry ring rather than the word being written -- a displacement of exactly 32 entries = 32 symbols, and mod-32 a lap-stale read and a lap-ahead read are the same displacement, which is why the measurement cannot and does not distinguish +32 from -32. LABELLING, kept honest: the LINE-LEVEL localisation above is [netlist] and certain; the cycle-level claim that this coincidence is what emits the lap-old word is [inferred] and NOT yet proven -- a behavioural replay of the RAM from h_m10_ring.txt did not converge inside this task's budget because that dump does not carry every beat. The falsifiable probe for the next task: dump in_re/in_im, wr_addr, rd_addr, valid_push, valid_pop and out_re/out_im on EVERY enb beat for +/-64 beats around a pop_on_empty and check whether the emitted word equals the ring content from the previous lap.

Task 7: CONTROLLER Q2 SETTLED — THE RESAMPLING SIGN IS NOT FLIPPED, AND THE "DRIFTS TOWARD FULL" READING WAS MINE, NOT THE DATA'S [sim]. (a) CODE: the --no-tile branch and the tiled branch compute the SAME `s = a.ppm * 1e-6` and call the SAME resample_periodic with the same time base `t = arange(...)*(1.0+s)` (gen_sro_stim.py:38,72,76 vs :111,112); the only difference is wrap=False (edge clamp) instead of modular indexing. (b) MEASURED, not read off the source: cross-correlating 8192-sample chunks of the resampled output back into the source and taking the mapping slope gives 1+0.000 ppm for --no-tile ppm=0 and 1-9.937 ppm for --no-tile ppm=-10 (out 197,328->2,713,260 maps to src 197,326->2,713,233). The output is time-STRETCHED at negative ppm, i.e. lower symbol rate, i.e. the ring must DRAIN -- the same convention as the tiled path. NO RE-RUN AT THE OPPOSITE SIGN IS NEEDED. (c) The apparent contradiction was an artefact of reading only the run totals. Per air frame (sm_b_m10_frames.txt cols 21,22,25,26): ALL 34 pop_on_empty are in frame 0 and ALL 17 push_on_full are in frame 2 -- the acquisition transient. From frame 3 the occupancy sits at 28-29 and DECLINES monotonically to 24-25 by frame 59, i.e. it drains at ~0.088 entries/frame against the 12333*1e-5 = 0.123 predicted (the occS/occE quantisation is coarse). Direction and magnitude are both right. (d) CONSEQUENCE, and it invalidates my own earlier "large H-A signal": the TGEN stimulus leaves the ring at occupancy ~28 after acquisition, not the 5 the tiled legs sit at, so at -10 ppm the EMPTY edge is ~28/0.123 = 228 frames away. The 60-frame smoke leg's 0.00 % loss therefore proves only that NO HOLE EVENT OCCURRED IN THE SCORED WINDOW -- it is not evidence for H-A. RETRACTED. The 430-frame legs do reach the edge at -10 ppm (~frame 230, then one event per 8.1 frames), and an accelerated -40 ppm leg reaches it at ~frame 57 and gives ~6 cycles; -2.5 ppm cannot reach an edge in 430 frames from occupancy 28 and will be reported as such rather than as a pass.

Task 7: RING-LAP MECHANISM REFUTED BY ITS OWN PROBE — I RETRACT IT [sim]. The per-enb-beat RAM-seam dump (sim_sro ramwin on s_m10.iq, 2 pop_on_empty windows, +/-64 beats, 259 rows, t7_ram_m10.txt) says: (a) `(wr - rd) mod 32` EQUALS the true occupancy at every beat in both windows (histograms identical: {0:49, 1:72, 2:8} and {0:49, 1:64, 2:16}) -- there is NO pointer/occupancy desync, the ring is never secretly holding 32 entries while reporting 0; (b) replaying the RAM behaviourally, the LAP DISTANCE of every emitted word is 0 or 1 pushes ago, NEVER 32, under both the read-before-write and the read-after-write model. So the ring emits current data and the "+32 = one lap of the 32-entry ring, read stale at the coincident address" mechanism I filed earlier is WRONG. It was labelled [inferred] and not built on; it is now withdrawn. (Caveat on the probe itself, stated: my replay model mismatches the emitted word on 14 of 30 validOut beats in each window because it does not reproduce the exact pipeline phase, so the model is not validated -- but the lap-distance histogram and the pointer-vs-occupancy identity do not depend on that phase and are direct readings.) WHAT SURVIVES: the measurement, unchanged -- the +32-symbol content step is real, one beat wide, and its beat is the pop_on_empty beat (sidx 1,997,338, tref 5976, occ 0). WHAT IS NOW OPEN AGAIN: what converts one suppressed valid slot into a 32-symbol content displacement. Both pop_on_empty windows here confirm occ=0 with wr=rd (29,29 and 24,24). No further mechanism is proposed from this desk.

Task 7: A LEG-DESIGN CONSEQUENCE THE CONTROLLER SHOULD SEE BEFORE THE VERDICT [sim]. The TGEN stimulus leaves the ring at occupancy ~28 after acquisition (measured, sm_b_m10_frames.txt), not the 5 the tiled legs sit at. Draining at 12333*|s| entries/frame, the EMPTY edge is therefore ~28/0.123 = 228 frames away at -10 ppm and ~910 frames away at -2.5 ppm. So a 430-frame -10 ppm leg reaches the edge near frame 230 and then runs ~25 hole cycles (one per 8.1 frames) -- a valid test -- but a 430-frame -2.5 ppm leg CANNOT REACH THE EDGE AT ALL and its 0.00 % would be vacuous, exactly the failure mode flagged earlier for +2.5. I am therefore adding an ACCELERATED -40 ppm baseline leg: edge at ~frame 57, then one hole per 2.03 frames = ~180 cycles in 430 frames. Predictions, pre-registered: if H-B (the death is real and content-independent) the -40 leg loses ~2 frames per cycle over the post-edge window, i.e. of order 80-100 %; if H-A (the death was a property of the tiled stimulus) it loses ~0 % while still showing the hole events in rh_pop_on_empty. The -2.5 leg is run and reported, but its result will be labelled VACUOUS unless rh_pop_on_empty > 0 inside the scored window.

Task 7: SILICON INSTRUMENT — MOST OF IT IS ALREADY IN THE FLASHED IMAGE, AND THE BLOCKER IS AN ADDRESS COLLISION [netlist]. In the flashed txfixF3 lineage the Rate_Handle mod-4 pop phase, the ring PUSH pointer and the ring POP pointer are already routed out of Symbol_Synchronizer (Frequency_and_Time_Synchronizer.v:263,265,267) into QPSK_Rx (BoDtc5/6/7_out1 at QPSK_Rx.v:650,652,654) and consumed by an existing witness block `BeatObs u_BeatObs` (QPSK_Rx.v:656-665). The in-tree comment at QPSK_Rx.v:676-678 records the read-back contract: witA = {2'b0, numEntries, push-pop delta, 2'b0} and witB = {push_on_full_count[31:16], delta_change_count[15:0]} at 0x20C / 0x210 -- i.e. TRUE OCCUPANCY and the push_on_full count were already host-readable. But QPSK_Rx.v:681-690 shows DBGCAP (2026-08-30) re-purposed 0x20C and 0x210 for its per-stage decision capture (dcap/dcmm). SO THE FIRST ACTION COSTS NOTHING: confirm ownership of 0x20C/0x210 on the flashed md5. If the ring witness still owns them the instrument is readable TODAY with no rebuild; if DBGCAP owns them, the fix is to move the address decode, not to add logic. Beyond that: ddrcap_sel = 12 is free (sels 0-11 used, TxRxComposite.v:2011-2033) and would carry {occupancy, push_ptr}/{pop_ptr, edge_event} at enb rate as a full-rate occupancy time series; and a per-stage valid census read through the existing cnt_mux32 freeze/sweep discipline localises the deleting stage by which stage's delta first falls short of 12,333*K. PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTION at the measured 2.5 ppm: rh_pop_on_empty increments once per 32.4 air frames (the PER comb period), every stage delta downstream of Rate_Handle is short by exactly that count, upstream stages exact, and both push_on_full counters stay 0. FALSIFIER: if all per-stage deltas are exact while frames still die at the comb period, the loss is NOT a symbol deletion and the whole SRO/Rate_Handle path is exonerated on silicon; and if rh_pop_on_empty stays 0 across several comb periods, every edge-directed fix (R1, R2, R3 and any successor) is aimed at the wrong place. Written into task-7-report.md section 6.

Those two nets are driven by QPSK_Rx.v:816,818 `fixctl[13] ? mdcapl : dcapl` / `... : dcmm` -- DBGCAP/DEMODCAP -- and further overridden at TxRxComposite.v:1975,2003 by `fixctl[12] ? txcapl/txcmm : ...` (TXCAP). BeatObs (TxRxCompo_ip_src_BeatObs.v:80-86,101-113) computes only `(pushc-popc) & 255` -- a POINTER DELTA -- and packs it into the RX I/Q debug sample stream; it has no numEntries input and no push_on_full counter. A pointer delta cannot distinguish occupancy 0 from 32, the same defect as sim_sro.cpp:115. True occupancy, pop_on_empty_FIFO and push_on_full_FIFO were NEVER in the image (survey sec 1 agrees). W1 must add the logic, not move an address.

  b_m2p5  (-2.5 ppm): seq 5..424, expect 420, OK 420, CORRUPT 0, MISSING 0, LOSS 0.00 %. rh_pop_on_empty 34 (all frame 0), **0 in the scored window** -> **VACUOUS**, exactly as pre-registered. rh_push_on_full 18 = 17 in frame 2 + 1 in frame 3, all acquisition. After acquisition occTrue pins at 31 and at 2.5 ppm drains 0.031 entries/frame, so the EMPTY edge is ~1000 frames away: this leg never reaches an edge and its 0.00 % says nothing about H-A or H-B.
  b_m10   (-10 ppm) : seq 4..424, expect 421, OK 375, CORRUPT 8, MISSING 38, **LOSS 10.93 %**. rh_pop_on_empty **55 = 34 acquisition (all in frame 0) + 21 INSIDE the scored window**, at frames 259,267,275,283,291,299,308,316,324,332,340,348,356,364,372,381,389,397,405,413,421 -- one per 8.1 frames, the predicted 1/(12333*|s|). rh_push_on_full **17, ALL in frame 2 (acquisition), 0 in the scored window**. occTrue drains 31 -> 0, first hole at frame 259 against 31/0.123 = 252 predicted.

EVIDENCE THAT SETTLES IT: (a) the loss ONLY appears once the ring actually reaches the EMPTY edge -- 0.00 % for 250 frames at -10 ppm while occupancy drains, then the comb starts within 4 frames of the first hole; (b) THE HOLES AND THE LOSSES ARE THE SAME EVENTS: at a constant seq<->frame offset of -3, **42 of the 46 lost frames sit within +/-1 of a hole**, and the per-cycle count is **2.10 lost frames per hole event** -- task 6's "two straddling frames die per cycle" law, reproduced on content that never repeats; (c) the post-edge loss rate is 44 losses in the 169 frames after the edge = **26.0 %**, against the matched TILED control tb_m10's **22.01 %** on the same harness -- the same magnitude, not a suppressed one; (d) the -40 ppm leg lands at 78.1 %, inside the pre-registered H-B band (order 80-100 %) and nowhere near the H-A prediction of ~0 %.

Task 7: CADENCE FIDELITY — ANSWERED BEFORE THE VERDICT IS FINALISED, AND IT DOES NOT WEAKEN IT [netlist]. The question is whether the harness reproduces silicon's tick/valid relationship at the receiver input. It does, and the reason is that the relationship is NOT stimulus-dependent: it is hard-tied in the netlist. TxRxComposite.v:476-481 --
    assign IntValidConst_out1 = 1'b1;
    assign RxValidConst_out1  = 1'b1;
    assign MUX_RxValid_out1 = (rx_input_select == 1'b0 ? IntValidConst_out1 : RxValidConst_out1);
-- and TxRxComposite.v:721 `.validIn(MUX_RxValid_out1)` feeds the Receiver. So in BOTH input modes, in sim and on silicon alike, the RX chain's validIn is CONSTANT 1 on every enb_1_2_0 tick. adc_validIn never gates the DSP chain; the ADC port is a rate-adapting capture register. RESULTING VALID DENSITY: at the receiver INPUT it is 1 valid per enb tick = 100 % in both cases, NOT 25 %. The "1 in 4" lives one stage later and is Rate_Handle's own mod-4 POP COUNTER (Rate_Handle.v:112-114, pop = validIn & (HDL_Counter_out1 == 0)) -- a counter, not an enable, exactly as RATE_HANDLE_FIX_SURVEY 1 states. The 1-in-4 that Preamble_Detector's Delay10_reg tick-delayed pop actually depends on is the Rate_Handle OUTPUT density, and that is reproduced exactly and was MEASURED in these very legs: the per-frame valid census gives ss = corr = 12,333 per 49,332-tick air frame (12,332 on the frames carrying a pop_on_empty), i.e. precisely 1 valid per 4 enb ticks, which is the condition under which 49,332 ticks == 12,333 valids. The harness's "cadence 2" setting governs only how often the C++ driver presents a NEW sample into that capture register: cadence 2 = one new sample per enb tick, which is what a 4-sps ADC stream does. That this is the correct choice is falsifiable and was falsified the other way (COMB32_SRO_SIM.md 5, measured): at cadence 2 the design's own rate arithmetic is satisfied -- 12,333 pushes = 12,333 pops per air frame, packets decode -- while at cadence 4 it was 23,895 pushes/pops per frame and ZERO packets decoded. CONCLUSION: the time-domain consumers (Delay10_reg's 49,332-tick delay, the Peak_Search / Timing_Adjust / End_Generator valid-counted epochs) see the same tick stream and the same 1-in-4 valid density as on silicon, so the verdict is stated as "H-B: the death mechanism is real and content-independent", NOT as the weaker "the harness cannot reproduce the silicon death on real content".

Task 11: THE SMOKE "FAIL" WAS MY OWN COMPARATOR, NOT THE RTL -- CAUSE NAMED, FIXED, SMOKE NOW PASSES [sim]. The 25-frame smoke leg delivered 21 frames; 19 of them are FULL-LINE identical to task 7's banked b_p000 (sidx column included) and ALL 21 are identical in every content column (nwords, the FNV hash over the delivered bytes, and the user flag: 191,3448b864,1 and 191,aa4ef66d,1 on the two tail frames in BOTH runs). The <p>_seq.txt records agree likewise -- seq 20 and 21, nbad=0, ok=1 in both. The ONLY difference is column 1 on the last two frames: 1233300/1233300 (smoke) against 1233333/1282665 (b_p000). CAUSE, in the driver and not in the netlist: sim_sro.cpp's sidx is the INPUT-SAMPLE counter and it FREEZES at nsamp once the stimulus runs out ("if(sidx<nsamp){...sidx++;} else t->adc_validIn=0;"), while frames already in the receive pipeline keep being delivered. The smoke is a TRUNCATED run (25 of 428 air frames), so its last delivered frames carry a frozen annotation where the full-length baseline carries the true one. Taking a whole-file md5 across that boundary tested the driver's bookkeeping, not the RTL -- an apples-to-oranges comparison I wrote. t11_chain.sh now checks (a) FULL-LINE identity, sidx included, for every frame delivered while the stimulus was still flowing and (b) content identity for every delivered frame; both PASS. A second, separate bug in the same script explains the contradictory log: the "matches b_p000 prefix" echo was unconditional and printed even after the FAIL; it is gone. NOTHING LEAKS INTO THE DATA PATH: wrapper provenance certified (WRAP3S_FILE wrap_byte_sro3s.v e7c1, WRAP3S_DEFINE RXFIX_R3S), r3_skips=0 r3_extras=0 (never armed, as predicted at 0 ppm), rhPE=34 rhPF=0 and capout=a037d28a identical to b_p000. NO STAGEWIN DUMP WAS NEEDED because there is no differing stage to name. G1 IN THE PRE-REGISTRATION IS UNCHANGED AND NEEDS NO SUCH CARE: the gate leg s_p000 runs nsamp=21,114,096, byte-for-byte the same as b_p000, so there is no truncation skew and the whole-file md5 identity stands exactly as pre-registered. FOUR GATE LEGS NOW LAUNCHED: t11_p000 / t11_m10 / t11_m40 (428 frames each) and t11_tm10 (165 frames, tiled control), prefixes s_p000 / s_m10 / s_m40 / s_tm10.

Task 10: STEP 0 DONE (desk/DRY, no board contact). New: two_jup/rxfix/w1leg_go.sh (MODE=ctrl|air), w1_score.py (w1_reads.csv cumulative+delta with mod-2^32/2^16 wrap arithmetic, structural decode checks, P/F evaluation), w1_ctl.py (Step-2 control table per tap). w1_read.sh gains two backward-compatible knobs: AUX (extra addresses swept in the SAME ssh round trip -- 0x104/0x124 -- so the frame count is paired with the frozen census) and HOLD (sleep between the two frozen sweeps = the 10 s freeze-path control). The DEFAULT register sequence on the wire is byte-for-byte unchanged and is now PINNED by a test. 31 tests green in two_jup/tests/test_rxfix_rig_scripts.py (13 pre-existing + 18 new). THREE DESIGN FINDINGS BEFORE ANY BOARD CONTACT. (1) THE IN-WINDOW READER MUST BE SEQUENTIAL, NOT TWO BACKGROUND READERS: the modem register window is read through ONE shared address-select register (addr_decoder.v:199), so two concurrent ssh sessions each doing `echo <addr> > $DRA; cat $DRA` interleave and each cats the OTHER's address -- silently, with a plausible value. slackleg_go.sh's pattern of backgrounding stage3h_reader.sh alongside the leg is safe only because it is the sole DRA user in the window; here w1_read.sh (16-18 addrs) and seqbist_read.py (0x104/0x124) would collide at a common 10 s cadence. w1leg_go.sh runs them one after the other in one loop; freeze_effective would have caught most W1-side corruption after the fact, but detectable is not avoided. (2) THE BRIEF'S CENSUS TOLERANCE "+-1 frame against 0x104" IS NOT ACHIEVABLE and not for noise reasons: 0x104 is NOT behind the W1 freeze, so it is sampled aux_lag_s after the frozen census. Split into a STRONG exact test (the five pre-discard census deltas equal each other -- one coherent snapshot, no pairing error) and a WEAK bounded one (cSS/12333 vs 0x104, tolerance COMPUTED per reading as rate x aux_lag_s from board-side timestamps now emitted by the reader). (3) cPC IS EXPECTED TO BE SHORT BY 13 PER FRAME by design (sample_discard_controller drops the inter-frame guard; W1_REGMAP sec 3), which CONTRADICTS P2's "every downstream stage delta equal to it" -- pre-registered separately as 12,320 x frames so a designed guard-drop is not read as evidence for P2-alt. Also pre-registered BEFORE the window: a THIRD BRANCH outside the brief's P/F set -- occupancy pinned near 32 with push_on_full incrementing on a FORWARD leg is a sign error in the campaign model, not "the FULL-edge counter finally worked". Scorers validated against four synthetic sets (P2, P2-alt, sign-error, const_0) and two ctrl sets (live/dead pop_on_empty); each is discriminated correctly. FIXCTL_BASE=0x0 justified by evidence, not assertion: arm148_mode1.sh writes '0x208 0x0' in every arm, and bringup_r2r3.sh/capture_r3.sh contain NO 0x208 write at all (grepped).

Task 12: RXFIX_R4 CUT [sim]. Injector variant R4 added to two_jup/skidfix/rxfix_inject.py (same seven internal modules as R3/R3S, marker RXFIX_R4, no new TxRxComposite/IP port). TWO pieces, not one: (a) PRE-FILL -- every pop is suppressed after reset until the registered ring occupancy has reached 16 once (r4_prefilled, sticky flop), costing a constant ~16 symbols of latency; (b) STEER -- while pre-filled AND locked, the first nominal pop per inter-frame guard window at occupancy <= 8 is skipped, at most one per frame, nothing on the FULL side. LOCK IS IN THE PREDICATE DELIBERATELY (the brief said "armed by prefilled (i.e. from lock)"): pre-fill completes inside air frame 0 but lock is ~air frame 5, and guardIn is stuck at 1 in that gap, so a stray skip would latch r4_skip_done -- which clears only on ~guardIn -- and silently disable the steering for the whole run. The lock term can only REMOVE skips. THE BRIEF'S MECHANISM SENTENCE IS PARTLY FALSIFIED BY TASK 7'S OWN BANKED DATA AND IS CORRECTED IN THE PRE-REGISTRATION RATHER THAN COPIED: "the steady-state occupancy is ~16 regardless of the acquisition transient" is FALSE on b_m10, whose air frame 2 goes occupancy 1 -> 31 with push_on_full = 17 (the acquisition burst fills the ring to FULL and deletes 17 pushes), so that leg's post-acquisition occupancy is ~31 whatever the ring was pre-filled to; b_m40 and tb_m10 have push_on_full = 0 and there the pre-fill does survive. R4 is therefore two claims: pre-fill owns the s = 0 INERTNESS (it lifts the 0 ppm operating point from occupancy 1/2 to 16/17, which is exactly what R3's bare occupancy predicate could not have), and the occ <= 8 threshold owns the steering wherever acquisition leaves the ring. IDENTITY AT s = 0 IS MEASURED, NOT STRUCTURAL, AND THAT IS A REAL DIFFERENCE FROM R3S: R3S ANDed its skip with r3s_armed so its unarmed pop was LITERALLY the baseline line; R4 gates the pop on r4_prefilled, so R4's pop expression is never the baseline expression and the 0 ppm gate row is CONTENT identity (per-frame nwords/FNV/user flag) plus a constant pre-fill latency. FAILURE MODE STATED AND IT IS WORSE THAN R3S'S: R3S failed toward baseline, R4 fails toward NO OUTPUT if the ring never reaches 16 (there is no timeout). WHERE THE SKIP LANDS, identical beat-for-beat on both legs: End_Generator fires at rel -4 (pcEnd=1), sdcAct drops 1->0 and guard rises 0->1, and the suppressed pop is THREE BEATS INSIDE THE GUARD BAND with sdcAct=0 and pcV=0 -- the deframer is consuming nothing at that instant; the whole guard/sdcAct/pcStart/pcEnd sequence is identical between the two windows and popEmpty is 0 on every beat of both. VALID CENSUS over the 129-beat window, event 1 (clean reference), identical on both legs: Rate_Handle.validOut 32->31, Coarse_Frequency_Compensator.validOut 32->31, Carrier_Synchronizer.validOut 32->31, Correlator.validOut 32->31, Preamble_Detector.validOut 33->33 (it is the TICK-delayed pop, the known tick-vs-valid divergence, not a reaction), Packet_Controller.validOut 19->19. So the deficit propagates ONE-FOR-ONE and NO STAGE DELETES OR INSERTS A SYMBOL OF ITS OWN. Control signals taRef 17..49, taAcc 23, psToff 23, toffVal 0, taArmed, taSync, pcStart, pcEnd, sdcAct and guard are EVERY ONE IDENTICAL between reference and skip window; the only movements are psTref's window top exactly one lower (5..37 -> 5..36, the one-symbol epoch slip R3S explicitly does not claim to remove) and the running content-dependent statistics Correlator.threshold / Peak_Search.p1c_runmax / cfcEst. THE DUMP GAVE A FREE CONTROL: event 0's reference window is one air frame before the FIRST skip, i.e. the ARMING-HOLE FRAME ITSELF (m10 260.010 vs 259.010, tiled 41.008 vs 40.008), and against that frame the same comparison DOES differ -- psToff 55->23, psNewpk collapsing to 0, pcEnd and pcV moving, sdcAct stuck at 0 across the whole reference window -- but those belong to the HOLE, not the skip. Beside event 1 that is the mechanism in one picture: a hole in the payload window disturbs Peak_Search and the deframer; a skip in the guard band disturbs nothing. FIRST STAGE WHOSE BEHAVIOUR DIFFERS ACROSS A STEERED SKIP: NONE. Task 10: TWO INSTRUMENT FACTS THE CONTROLS EXPOSED, FOLDED IN BEFORE THE AIR LEG. (1) pop_on_empty CLEARS ON EVERY ARM: at rest it read 44, after arm #1 it read 10, after arm #2 it read 44 again -- the arm's 0x000 soft reset zeroes it and it then accumulates that acquisition's own transient (10 and 44 on two arms; sim 34). So only DELTAS between in-window reads are meaningful and a cumulative value is never comparable across an arm. The scorer already uses per-interval deltas. (2) INTER-STAGE EQUALITY NEEDS A MEASURED TOLERANCE, NOT EXACT EQUALITY: the stages sit at FIXED pipeline offsets in any frozen snapshot -- cRH=cSS-1, cCFC=cSS-9, cPD=cSS-12352 constant across all 9 snapshots -- while cCS alternates cSS-11/cSS-12, one beat of sampling phase. A constant offset cancels in a delta; the one-beat jitter does not, so a stage delta can differ from cSS's by +-1 with ZERO loss (proven: zero SRO, pop_on_empty delta 0). EQ_TOL set to +-4 from this measurement. This matters because P2-alt predicts a shortfall of ~395 per 10 s, ~100x the band -- the two are never confusable, but exact equality would have failed P2 spuriously on every reading. Measured cPD offset 12,352 vs the sim's ~12,340.

Task 12: SMOKE FAILED ON MY OWN CHAIN, NOT ON THE RTL -- CAUSE NAMED, RULE CORRECTED, FIVE GATE LEGS NOW LAUNCHED [sim]. t12_chain.sh (v1) reported T12_SMOKE_FAIL and did not launch anything. THREE causes, two of them mine. (1) WRONG CONSTANT: v1 tested the r4_prefilled sentinel as decimal 2779087873; 0xA5A50001 IS 2779054081, which is exactly what the run reported -- the sentinel was right all along and the pre-fill did happen. (2) GATE ROWS IMPORTED INTO A SMOKE: v1 required r4_skips = 0 and occupancy in [14,18] -- G2 and G3 of the pre-registration -- on a 25-air-frame TRUNCATED run that is almost entirely acquisition. Those rows are scored on the FULL-LENGTH leg and reported there, pass or fail; a smoke exists to prove the harness compiled the right wrapper and the RTL delivers the right bytes before five full legs are spent. (3) THE sidx-DELTA SPREAD IS THE TASK 11 TRUNCATION LESSON AGAIN, and it is not a smoke condition: sim_sro.cpp's sidx freezes at nsamp while frames already in the pipeline keep being delivered, so the tail of a truncated run carries a frozen annotation -- measured delta -49365, which is one air frame (49332) plus 33, i.e. the freeze, NOT content moving. Smoke rule is now wrapper provenance + SEQ-KEYED content identity only (t12_chain2.sh, a new file so what v1 actually ran stays auditable); everything else prints as a non-blocking diagnostic. Smoke re-evaluated on the SAME artifacts and PASSED: WRAP4_FILE/WRAP4_DEFINE present, 20/20 common seq content-identical (nwords/FNV/user), capout a037d28a identical to b_p000. THE SMOKE DIAGNOSTICS ARE A REAL AND UNWELCOME FINDING, PRE-EMPTING G2/G3, AND IT FALSIFIES THE BRIEF'S MECHANISM ON THE 0 PPM LEG TOO -- NOT JUST ON b_m10. Measured per-frame occupancy: air frame 0 pre-fills to oMax=17 (the pre-fill works), but the acquisition transient then DRAINS IT COMPLETELY and still takes 23 pop_on_empty (baseline 34), so by air frame 1 the ring is back at 0/1 and frames 1-12 track b_p000 EXACTLY at occupancy 1/2. THE ACQUISITION DEFICIT AT 0 PPM IS ~34 ENTRIES, LARGER THAN THE 16 PRE-FILLED AND LARGER THAN THE 32-DEEP RING, so no pre-fill depth could have survived it. What then defines the operating point is the STEERING, not the pre-fill: r4_locked comes true at air frame 13 (eight deframer frames after the first at ~frame 5, exactly as designed), the occ <= 8 predicate fires once per frame for frames 13-20 (8 skips), occupancy ratchets 1->9, and at 9 > 8 the predicate goes false and the ring sits FLAT at 9/10 with pop_on_empty = 0 for the rest of the run. So R4 does lift the ring off the EMPTY edge at 0 ppm -- by steering, and at the cost of 8 skips where the pre-registration predicted 0. G2 (r4_skips = 0) and G3 (occupancy in [14,18]) are therefore expected to FAIL on the full leg and will be reported as failures, not reinterpreted. G1 IS UNAFFECTED AND THE 8 SKIPS COST NOTHING IN DELIVERED FRAMES: content is identical on every common seq. THE PRE-FILL LATENCY IS NOT A SINGLE CONSTANT AND THE MEASUREMENT SAYS WHY: the sidx delta distribution is 0:8, then 4,8,12,16,20,24,28 one frame each, then 32:3 -- a clean ramp in steps of 4 input samples = ONE SYMBOL per unit of ring-occupancy difference, tracking the occupancy climbing 1->9 over frames 13-21. delta = 4 x (occ_R4 - occ_baseline), predicted to plateau at 32 samples (8 symbols), NOT the +64 (16 symbols) I pre-registered -- because the pre-fill that would have bought 16 symbols was consumed by acquisition. The full-length leg measures the plateau over frames delivered while the stimulus was still flowing and the report states the distribution honestly. FIVE LEGS RUNNING: t12_p000 / t12_m10 / t12_m40 / t12_tm10 / t12_p10, prefixes r4_p000 / r4_m10 / r4_m40 / r4_tm10 / r4_p10, nsamp verified identical to the banked baselines before launch. Task 12b: DESIGN CHANGE ACKNOWLEDGED AND ADOPTED -- THE PRE-FILL IS DROPPED FROM R4B [sim]. Controller ruling 2026-09-04T17:07:50 (from Task 12's own smoke diagnostics, ledger line above): the 0 ppm acquisition deficit is ~34 entries -- larger than the 16 the pre-fill buys AND larger than the 32-deep ring -- so NO pre-fill depth survives acquisition; what actually defines the operating point is the STEERING (after lock the occ <= 8 predicate fires once per frame, ratchets occupancy 1 -> 9 in ~8 frames, then the predicate goes false and the ring sits flat at 9/10 with pop_on_empty = 0). R4B is therefore: LOCK (8 pcEnd pulses) -> STRUCTURAL-WINDOW skip at occ <= 8 -> SELF-CENTRED at 9/10. Consequences carried through: (a) r4b_prefilled and the brief's bounded-pre-fill timeout (review item 4) are DELETED, not weakened -- the report will name the deletion and its justification rather than omit it; (b) the pop expression is r4b_pop_nom & ~r4b_skip_en, so before the first arm it IS literally the baseline expression again (R3S's structural s=0 property, which R4 had lost by gating the pop on r4_prefilled) -- but the 0 ppm gate row is still CONTENT identity, because the ~8 startup skips shift sidx; (c) the R4B fail mode returns to R3S's: fail-toward-BASELINE, not R4's fail-toward-NO-OUTPUT; (d) the ninth-word witness loses r4b_prefilled, and armed == locked in this design, so the word is {r4b_locked, r4b_skips[15:0], r4b_window_opens[14:0]} and the W1_REGMAP liveness positive control becomes "r4b_locked = 1 and r4b_window_opens advancing at the frame rate"; (e) the n_p000 gate row is pre-registered as the controller states it -- content identity, ~8-10 skips inside the first ~10 frames after lock, then r4b_skips FLAT with pop_on_empty = 0 -- NOT the brief's "r4b_skips = 0". Threshold kept at occ <= 8 (brief item 7 made it conditional on Task 12's R4 gate; the 17:07 diagnostics are that evidence and they arrived before any R4B leg). Everything else in the brief stands: structural pcEnd window, _PFX anchors + kit test, registered decision, pcEnd lock, ninth W1 word, CFO / +10 ppm / re-lock legs. NO BOARD CONTACT.

Leg gate PASSED (capture_r3_exit=0, deliver_rate 1900 f/s pre AND post, 0 watchdog relaunches, wedge_verdict healthy crc=92%); 48 reads / 47 intervals, spacing min=max=10.0 s; no re-run needed. P1 HOLDS: occ in {0,1} on every read, pop_on_empty delta mean 394.0 per 10 s (predicted 395 +- 60), push_on_full delta 0 on every interval. P2 HOLDS: cSS==cRH everywhere and all five pre-discard stages equal within the measured +-4 band, max deviation observed 2, where P2-alt would need -394; per-stage means cRH -0.021, cCFC -0.021, cCS -0.021, cPD +0.000. P2-ALT REFUTED: 0 of 188 stage-readings short by the pop_on_empty delta. P3 HOLDS all three clauses: checker gap events 656.2 per 10 s = 1.67x pop_on_empty (predicted 1-2x); PER 8.309 % (72,738/875,375, lost frames in the denominator, CP95UL 8.367) within 8 +- 1 %; lag32 +0.6130 against a 0.0039 null; comb_period_ms 25.3872 within 25-26. F1 does not fire, F3 does not fire, THIRD BRANCH does not fire (occupancy is at the EMPTY edge as modelled, so the sign question in RXFIX_STATE 2(a) is NOT resolved by this leg). THE DECISIVE NUMBER: pop_on_empty mean inter-event interval 25.3793 ms (18,519 events over 470 s) vs the independently measured PER comb period 25.3872 ms -- agreement 0.031 %, implied SRO 2.565 ppm. The ring's empty-edge event and the PER comb are the SAME event, from two instruments sharing no code path, and the census proves that event deletes NOTHING. WRAP ACCOUNTING: census 1.68 x 2^32 each over the window, pop_on_empty 0.28 x 2^16, push_on_full 0; census wrap horizon 279 s and edge horizon 1659 s, no interval reaches either, every delta accumulated from consecutive 10 s reads and never leg-end minus leg-start. UNEXPLAINED: cPC runs 0.266 % below even its designed 12,320/frame guard drop -- ratio 0.084 to pop_on_empty (P2-alt would need 1.000) and not the 8.309 % PER either.

Task 13: PHASE A INSTRUMENT WORK DONE; PARKED ON THE BUILD, NO BOARD CONTACT [netlist]. Reader/scorer now handle the ninth word and THE FREEZE TRAP IS AVOIDED: 0x234 is deliberately NOT behind fixctl[4] and r4b_window_opens advances at the FRAME rate, so folding it into freeze_effective would have marked every reading of a live board incoherent -- and Task 9's rule discards those, i.e. it would have thrown away the whole air leg. freeze_effective stays over the EIGHT frozen words; the ninth moving inside the freeze window is instead its liveness control (r4b_moved), with tests on both halves. Field order taken from the RTL ({r4b_locked, r4b_skips[15:0], r4b_opens[14:0]}), not from the 12b brief whose 34-bit field list predates the 17:07 pre-fill ruling; r4b_opens is 15 bits so its deltas use mod 2^15 (M16 would corrupt every opens delta across a wrap). WRAP HORIZON CORRECTED BEFORE THE FIRST READ: W1_REGMAP said r4b_opens wraps in ~136 s "at 240 f/s", but this rig ran ~1,260 f/s on Task 10's own leg (0x104 ~12,600 per 10 s), so the true horizon is 2^15/1260 = ~26 s -- the FASTEST-wrapping quantity in the instrument (census 279 s, edge counters 1,659 s). 10 s reads clear it by 2.6x, one dropped read still does, two consecutive dropped reads alias silently; the scorer checks every interval. FAIL-CLOSED GUARD ADDED: w1leg_go.sh's EXP defaults to the W1 image 2728dab3979a, so an R4B leg on the default would have PASSED the image gate on W1 silicon, read const_0 at 0x234 and scored T13-P1/F-B against a board with no R4B in it; R4B=1 now requires an explicit EXP and refuses the W1 md5. Task 10's own air readings re-score identically after every edit (P1 HOLDS, occ {0,1}, pop_on_empty 394.0/10 s, F2 FIRES). Tests: 123/123 injector, 36/36 rig-script. Commits e186fa7, b9739e9, 1be33ee. Build still running (synth); Phase B remains gated on "Task 12b: complete" with the gate PASS.

Task 12: GATE RESULT — 7 OF 11 PASS; G4 AND G10 (THE TWO ROWS R3S FAILED) BOTH PASS; G2, G3, G7, G9 FAIL [sim]. RAW NUMBERS, all five legs on the R4 build (task 7's sim_sro.cpp unmodified, nsamp identical to the banked baselines, baselines NOT re-run). G1 PASS and stronger than required: r4_p000 is CONTENT-identical to b_p000 on 423/423 common seq with identical seq sets, and packets=424, biterr=23120, capout=a037d28a, nrxw=80984 all match exactly. G2 FAIL r4_skips=8 (pre-registered 0), at air frames 13-20. G3 FAIL occupancy 9/10 (pre-registered [14,18]). G4 PASS r4_m10 LOSS 0.48 % (2 of 421) against <= 0.5 %, and the two lost frames are seq 133,134 EXACTLY as the row requires -- task 7's inherited, non-hole-aligned pair, lost in the baseline too; baseline b_m10 10.93 %, R3S 0.95 %. G5 PASS 29 skips (band 15-35), ALL 29 at tref 21 (<= 60 slots from the epoch boundary). G6 PASS rh_pop_on_empty in the scored window = 0 (23 total, all air frame 0). G7 FAIL r4_m40 LOSS 4.48 % against <= 1 % (405 of 424 byte-exact; baseline 78.07 %; R3S also 4.48 % -- R4 does NOT improve -40 ppm). G8 PASS 210 skips (band 150-260). G9 FAIL 7 pop_on_empty in the scored window at air frames 6,8,10,12,14,16,18 -- every one BEFORE the first steered skip at frame 19. G10 PASS r4_tm10 LOSS 0.00 % (159 of 159; baseline tb_m10 22.01 %, R3S 1.89 %) with the ring running 16 -> 8 and touching NEITHER edge for the whole run. G11 PASS on all five legs. THE FALSIFIER DID NOT FIRE, AND LESS THAN IT DID FOR R3S: 0 of 29 skips have a loss within +/-1 and 0.00 lost frames per skip, against the baseline's 2.00 per hole; R3S still had one loss-adjacent skip (the one after its arming hole), R4 has none because it has no arming hole. WHY G2/G3 FAIL, AND IT IS THE FINDING: the pre-fill works (r4_prefilled sets at input sample 79, ring reaches oMax=17 in air frame 0) but ACQUISITION THEN CONSUMES IT ENTIRELY -- the 0 ppm acquisition deficit is ~34 ring entries, which is more than the 16 pre-filled AND MORE THAN THE 32-DEEP RING, so no pre-fill depth could survive it; by air frame 1 the ring is back at 1/2 and frames 1-12 track b_p000 exactly. What sets the operating point is the STEERING: lock at air frame 13, occ <= 8 fires once per guard window for frames 13-20, occupancy ratchets 1 -> 9, the predicate goes false and the ring sits FLAT at 9/10 with pop_on_empty = 0 for the remaining 407 frames. The brief's "steady-state occupancy ~16 regardless of the acquisition transient" holds on exactly ONE of four legs (the tiled control, push_on_full=0 and pop_on_empty=0 for the entire run, ring 16 -> 8, neither edge touched). PRE-FILL LATENCY MEASURED AND IT IS NOT WHAT I PRE-REGISTERED: the per-seq sidx delta is 0 for seq 2-9, a ramp of exactly 4 input samples (ONE SYMBOL) per frame across seq 10-16 -- the eight steering events -- then a FLAT +32 input samples = 8 symbols for seq 17-422, i.e. all 406 frames delivered while the stimulus was still flowing (the last two read 0 because sidx freezes at nsamp in BOTH runs, task 11's truncation artifact at the tail of a full-length run). delta = 4 x (occ_R4 - occ_baseline) frame by frame. The pre-registered +64 (16 symbols) was wrong because the 8 symbols of standing occupancy are built by the SKIPS, not by the pre-fill. G7/G9 CAUSE IS LOCK LATENCY, NOT THE ARMING RULE: at -40 ppm the ring is at the EMPTY edge from frame 3 while r4_locked cannot be true until eight deframer frames have passed, so seven holes are taken at frames 6-18 before the first skip at 19; after that the steering takes over completely and there is NOT ONE further hole in the remaining 409 frames. R3S had the identical structure and the identical residual.
Task 12: +10 ppm OBSERVATION (report only, not a gate row) [sim]. r4_p10 LOSS 6.41 % (27 of 421), rh_push_on_full=27, occTrue_end=32; the ring climbs 0 -> 32 and reaches the FULL edge at air frame 210, then deletes one push every ~8 frames. THE LOSSES ARE THE DELETIONS: aligning the 27 lost seq to the 27 push_on_full frames gives best constant offset -2 with 27/27 losses within +/-1 (100 %), 24 of 27 events with a loss beside them, 1.12 lost frames per deletion (the brief expected ~2). A push_on_full DELETES a symbol, unlike a pop_on_empty which only skips a slot, so this is a different failure mode from the comb R4 fixes. THE SKIP-ONLY RULE IS MILDLY COUNTER-PRODUCTIVE HERE AND THE SIGN IS WORTH RECORDING: R4 took 6 skips at frames 13-18 while the ring was still climbing, each adding an entry to a ring that is already filling, bringing the FULL edge ~49 frames closer. THE 167 pop_on_empty ON THIS LEG ARE PURE ACQUISITION AND NOTHING TO DO WITH POSITIVE SRO: 23 in air frame 0, 48 in frame 1, 96 in frame 2, ZERO from frame 3 onward, and the first delivered seq is 5 so none is in the scored window; every leg shows the same 23-event frame-0 signature (the pre-fill/drain) and p10 simply has a longer acquisition transient. Task 12b's b_p10 baseline was NOT available at scoring time (t12b_bp10 still running, no b_p10_res.txt), so the +10 ppm leg is an observation and not a comparison.
Task 12: complete. Report two_jup/sdd_archive/2026-09-04-rxfix/task-12-report.md; pre-registration two_jup/comb/RXFIX_R4_SIM_GATE.md (committed before any leg, 7269cd8). VERDICT: R4's STEERING is the shippable part and the PRE-FILL is not -- the occ <= 8 guard-band skip produced 0.48 % at -10 ppm and 0.00 % on the tiled control and reaches its operating point unaided on every leg, while the pre-fill costs 8 symbols of latency, fails its own two gate rows, survives acquisition on only one leg of four, and introduces a FAIL-TO-SILENCE mode R3S did not have (no pop is ever taken if the ring never reaches 16; there is no timeout). That is the same conclusion Task 12b reached from my smoke diagnostics when it dropped the pre-fill for R4B. The remaining -40 ppm residual is a LOCK-LATENCY problem, not an arming problem: seven holes are taken before eight deframer frames have elapsed, so the next probe there is a cheaper lock predicate, not a different skip rule. Injector tests 96 green (74 + 22 TestR4); symmetric per-file mutual exclusion with R3/R3S both directions; W1 + R4 combined application PROVEN for task 13 (both orders, both lineages, identical sorted line multisets on all five shared files, combined tree lints error-free on both lineages); verilator --lint-only error-free on both lineages alone (r4_lint_s1_rtl.log, r4_lint_txfixF3.log, 0 %Error each). Heartbeat t12hb stopped. NO BOARD CONTACT AT ANY POINT.
Task 12: complete (accepted; review folded into the Task 12b gate review). Controller reading: the occ<=8 guard-band skip alone gives -10 ppm 0.48 % (only the inherited seq 133/134 pair), tiled 0.00 %, 0 of 29 skips loss-adjacent; the -40 ppm residual 4.48 % is entirely the 7 pre-lock holes (frames 6-18, before the first skip at 19) — an acquisition-window exposure that scales with |SRO| and is ~0 at 2.5 ppm (drain 0.03 entries/frame vs a ~21-frame lock+ratchet window from occupancy 1). +10 ppm: 27 push_on_full deletions each lose ~1.1 frames (FULL-edge problem for the reverse leg, unaddressed). Carried into R4B/Task 13.
Task 12: CORRECTION to the -40 ppm figure and the G4 margin, both in the report [sim]. (1) G7's LOSS was quoted as 4.48 % using Task 11's 424-frame denominator, but the pre-registration scores this leg by honest delivered/byte-exact count and r4_m40 DELIVERED 425 packets, so the figure is 405/425 = 4.71 %; 4.48 % is what the same byte-exact count gives on Task 11's denominator. Quoting the denominator that reproduces R3S's headline made "no improvement at -40 ppm -- 4.48 % both times" look like a measured equality when the two legs deliver DIFFERENT streams (md5 f877aeb6 vs 09e3c5ed) and only match because the denominator was chosen after seeing it match. Corrected to 4.71 % with both denominators shown; G7 FAILS on either, so the verdict is unchanged and only the provenance of the number improves. (2) G4 PASSES WITH NO HEADROOM and the report now says so: <= 0.5 % of 421 admits at most 2 lost frames, a third would be 0.71 % and a fail, and both losses are task 7's inherited seq 133/134 pair. Passed exactly as written, not comfortably.

Task 12b: CFO ANOMALY RESOLVED FROM BANKED DATA -- NEITHER (a) NOR (b): the +20 kHz CFO changes ONLY THE ACQUISITION TRANSIENT, and the steady-state drain is bit-identical to the no-CFO leg [sim]. THE DECISIVE NUMBER: total interpolator strobes (validated pushes + push_on_full) in air frames 3..428 are 5,266,472 on b_m10 AND 5,266,472 on b_m10c -- EQUAL TO THE STROBE. All 240 of the missing strobes are in air frames 0-2 (37,005 vs 36,765). The per-frame push distribution over the body of the run is likewise identical on both legs (372 frames at 12,333 and 53 at 12,332). And the hole COMB SPACING is identical: b_m10 holes every 8 frames (18 gaps of 8, 2 of 9) and b_m10c holes every 8 frames (46 of 8, 5 of 9) -- both exactly the -10 ppm drift of 12,333 x 10e-6 = 0.1233 entries/frame = one entry per 8.11 frames. SO THERE IS NO 46 ppm DRAIN AND NO CYCLE SLIPPING: hypothesis (b) (the timing loop slipping under +20 kHz in RTL) is REFUTED by a bit-exact steady-state strobe count, and hypothesis (a) (rotation at the wrong rate / clipping / CFC placement) is not right either -- the generator reported clip=0 and rms=8191.8, identical to n_m10's. WHAT ACTUALLY DIFFERS IS WHERE THE RING IS PARKED BY ACQUISITION, WHICH SETS WHEN THE COMB STARTS, NOT HOW FAST IT RUNS. b_m10 air frame 2 takes 12,364 strobes (31 EXTRA) with push_on_full = 17 -- task 7's and task 11's recorded acquisition BURST -- and parks the ring at 31/32, so it must drain 31 entries at 0.1233/frame = 251 frames before the first EMPTY edge: first hole at frame 259. b_m10c has NO burst (air frames 1-2 take 12,234 and 12,248 strobes with 100 and 85 holes) and parks the ring at 0/1, so the first hole is at frame 8. The whole 278 vs 55 difference decomposes as acquisition 226 vs 34 plus steady 52 vs 21, and BOTH steady numbers are just (421 - first_hole_frame)/8.11. This is the SAME phenomenon task 12 measured at 0 ppm and task 11 recorded for b_m10: the acquisition transient, not the CFO, owns the operating point. CONSEQUENCE FOR THE GATE: G12 is NOT vacuous and NOT an artefact -- the CFO leg is simply a HARDER leg (the ring sits on the EMPTY edge from frame 8 instead of frame 259), and R4B's result on it is the strongest in the gate: r4b_m10c's acquisition is BIT-IDENTICAL to b_m10c's (air frames 0-2: 41/100/85 holes, same strobe counts -- exactly as the structural pre-arm identity predicts, since the pop IS the baseline expression until lock) and then it takes EXACTLY ONE steady-state hole, at frame 8, against the baseline's 52. 51 of 52 steady holes pre-empted by 60 skips; the frame-8 hole is the arming hole (lock needs 8 pcEnd pulses, ~frame 5-13). CONTROL LEG LAUNCHED ANYWAY, PREDICTION RECORDED BEFORE READING IT: b_p000c = 0 ppm + 20 kHz CFO on task 7's UNMODIFIED baseline binary, 120 air frames (a ~12 min leg, inside the coordinator's 15 min budget). PREDICTED: a LARGE acquisition transient (order 150-250 pop_on_empty in air frames 0-2, like b_m10c's 226) and then ZERO pop_on_empty from air frame 3 on, because at 0 ppm there is no drift to drain the ring. IF INSTEAD A REGULAR COMB APPEARS AFTER FRAME 3, the CFO does drain the ring in the harness and hypothesis (b) revives. NO BOARD CONTACT.

Task 12b: GATE SCORED -- MECHANISM CONFIRMED, 15 of 17 GATES PASS, TWO REAL FAILURES REPORTED AS FAILURES [sim]. 356 steered skips across seven legs, EVERY ONE inside the structural window [pcEnd+1, pcEnd+13] and 356/356 at slot 1 (or 3 once), with the RTL window counter and an INDEPENDENT wrapper recount agreeing on all 356 and dt_enb = 4*slot-1 on every event; the skip count agrees three ways on every leg (trace lines = r3_skips = kind-4 ep records). LOSS: n_m10 10.93 -> 0.48 % (only the inherited seq 133/134), n_m40 76.98 -> 0.74 %, tiled 22.01 -> 0.00 % (R3S was 1.89 %), forced loss-of-lock 4.39 -> 0.24 %, CFO leg 28.33 -> 0.24 %. n_p000 content-identical on 423/423 common seq with identical seq sets, and PRE-ARM STRUCTURAL IDENTITY holds on EVERY paired leg (184/184 frames on n_m10) -- byte-identical INCLUDING sidx before the first skip, the property R4 had lost by gating its pop on r4_prefilled. The ring self-centres at 9/10 on all six negative-SRO legs (388/388 frames at 9/10 on n_p000) and pop_on_empty in the scored window is 0 on five of seven legs. G14 FAILS AND IT IS A REAL REGRESSION: on +10 ppm R4B loses 6.65 % against the baseline's 4.99 % -- 7 extra frames -- because its 7 acquisition-transient skips pushed an already-FULL ring (occupancy 31/32 measured) over the edge: push_on_full 21 -> 28 and validated pushes -7, ONE LOST FRAME PER SKIP. The occ <= 8 predicate can fire during the acquisition dip even on a positive-SRO link, where the steering is not merely useless but harmful; no fix is cut here (new variant, new gate) but the candidates are to inhibit the skip while push_on_full has fired recently, or to require the low occupancy to persist. G13 also fails as written (28.33 % vs the pre-registered 5-20 % band) but in the OPPOSITE direction to the vacuity it guarded against. THE CFO QUESTION IS SETTLED FROM BANKED DATA, NEITHER (a) NOR (b): steady-state strobes over air frames 3..428 are 5,266,472 on b_m10 AND 5,266,472 on b_m10c -- bit-identical -- with all 240 missing strobes in air frames 0-2, identical per-frame push distributions (372 at 12,333 / 53 at 12,332 on both) and identical comb spacing (one hole per 8.11 frames = exactly -10 ppm) on both; the stimulus measures 20,000.0 Hz with |ratio| = 1.000000 and zero clipped samples. The CFO only changes the ACQUISITION transient: b_m10's air frame 2 takes 31 EXTRA strobes with push_on_full = 17 and parks the ring at 31/32 (first hole frame 259), b_m10c has no burst and parks it at 0/1 (first hole frame 8). So there is NO 46 ppm drain and NO cycle slipping, G12 is a demanding rather than a vacuous row, and R4B pre-empts 51 of its 52 steady holes. THE tref FALSIFIER FIRED ON THREE LEGS AND IS REPORTED AS FIRED, then diagnosed as a MIS-CALIBRATED REFERENCE: the offsets are CONSTANT (all 60 m10c skips within 2 symbols of that leg's own modal tref; the 7 post-outage lol skips all at exactly 11693) where a false sync is a LONE OUTLIER (R3S's tref 7026 among 202 at 12295/12296), the skips are still at slot 1 of the deframer's own guard, and decisively NO FRAME DIES AT THEM -- 60 flagged skips cost one frame. Task 14: THE SPECIFIED R4C PREDICATE IS FALSIFIED BEFORE CUTTING ANYTHING, AND THE ERROR WAS MINE [sim]. Task 12b's report section 10 recommended 'inhibit the skip once push_on_full has ever fired' on the strength of my own FALSE statement that push_on_full is 0 on all six negative-SRO legs; it is 17 on n_m10. Banked per-frame data refutes the predicate in BOTH directions: on n_m10 the acquisition burst fires push_on_full 17 times at AIR FRAME 2, before lock, so a flag sticky from reset latches there and would suppress ALL 30 skips on the leg the variant exists for (forward loss reverting 0.48 -> ~10.93 %); on n_p10 the first push_on_full is at frame 202 while all 7 harmful skips are at frames 13-19, so the inhibit engages ~180 frames too late and removes NONE of them. THE TASK'S OWN GATE ROW PROVES THE CONTRADICTION: it requires byte-identity of r4c vs r4b on n_m10 'because push_on_full is 0 there', but it is 17 there, so under the specified predicate R4C would differ from R4B on exactly the leg the row asserts they must match. A post-lock version of the flag rescues n_m10 but still leaves all 7 n_p10 skips untouched. STRONGER RESULT -- THE WHOLE CLASS OF FIX IS RULED OUT: at the instant the first skip is decided the ring state is IDENTICAL in the benign, harmful and essential cases. The four air frames before the first skip read oMin/oMax = 1/2, 1/2, 1/2, 1/2 on BOTH n_p000 (benign, first skip frame 13 occ 1) and n_p10 (harmful, first skip frame 13 occ 2), with pe = 0 and pf = 0 on every one of them, and n_m10c (essential) at 0/1; the ratchets that follow are 1,3,3,5,6,6 and 2,3,4,5,7,8. Nothing in the ring state at decision time separates a link about to fill from one about to drain -- that information does not exist yet (at 10 ppm it takes ~8 frames per entry of drift to appear and ~57 frames to matter). So the task's three requirements -- R4C == R4B on the negative legs, zero harmful skips on n_p10, and a memoryless sticky-flag predicate -- CANNOT ALL BE MET; one must be dropped. TWO CANDIDATES PUT TO THE CONTROLLER in two_jup/comb/RXFIX_R4C_SIM_GATE.md with a pre-registered gate for the recommended one: (A) arm on a post-lock pop_on_empty (R3S's arming + R4B's structural window) -- n_p10 has ZERO pop_on_empty from air frame 3 on, so it never arms, takes 0 skips and equals the baseline by construction, at the cost of the arming hole (n_m10 ~0.95 % instead of 0.48 %, and NOT byte-identical to R4B anywhere); (B) observe the drift over ~16 windows before acting -- preserves R4B's steady state but delays every startup ratchet and needs a new shift register and its own gate; (C) no new variant, gate per direction, which is already in force via the controller's 148-only GO. RECOMMENDATION: a skip-only variant CANNOT help the 146 reverse leg at all -- its defect is the FULL edge, where push_on_full DELETES symbols, and R4B/R4C only ever ADD entries to a ring already against that edge; the best a reverse-leg R4C can do is nothing, which is exactly what Candidate A does. Improving 146 is a FULL-SIDE task, and R3's extra-pop branch was the previous attempt at that (task 7: 100 % loss of framing). AWAITING THE CONTROLLER'S CHOICE BETWEEN A, B AND C BEFORE CUTTING. No R4C code written. NO BOARD CONTACT; no 146 flash without the operator.

MEASURED on 120 air frames of n_p000c.iq with task 7's UNMODIFIED baseline binary: acquisition holes 41 + 96 + 97 = 234 (inside the band), 8 more in air frame 3 as the tail of the same transient, and ZERO in every one of the 117 air frames after it; push_on_full = 0; the ring settles at occupancy 1/2 and stays there; rh_pop_on_empty = 245 for the leg. Task 14: RXFIX_R4D CUT AND SEVEN GATE LEGS LAUNCHED [sim]. R4D = R4B + the FULL-side mirror: when occupancy >= 24 inside the SAME 13-slot structural window, take ONE EXTRA pop (emit one extra valid), at most one per pcEnd, registered exactly like the skip. Gate PRE-REGISTERED AND COMMITTED BEFORE ANY R4D CODE (3f33e42): two_jup/comb/RXFIX_R4D_SIM_GATE.md, J1-J11, and its section 1 MEASURES from banked task 12b data which legs can even differ -- max oMax after lock is 10 on n_p000, n_m40, the tiled leg, n_m10c and the loss-of-lock leg, so the >= 24 predicate is NEVER true there and R4D == R4B STRUCTURALLY on five of seven legs (J6's byte-identity is a prediction about the RTL text, not a hope); only n_m10 (post-burst 31, frames 11-73) and n_p10 (frames 129-428) can differ. RTL (s1_rtl_rxfix_R4D/Rate_Handle.v): the extra fires at mod-4 PHASE 2, i.e. on a beat where the nominal pop is not firing, so the instantaneous valid spacing goes 4,2,2 rather than putting two pops on adjacent beats; pop = (r4d_pop_nom & ~r4d_skip_en) | r4d_do_extra; both compares (occ <= 8 and occ >= 24) and both decisions (r4d_skip_en, r4d_extra_en) are REGISTERED, so the only combinational path into the pop is still the baseline's own guard; the two edges cannot both be armed (dead band 9..23, fifteen entries wide). WHY THIS IS NOT R3'S EXTRA POP (task 7: r3_extras = 4 with 100 % loss of framing): R3's extras fired under a 'deframer idle' predicate -- true after a false sync, a missed sync and throughout acquisition -- with no lock term, so they landed outside any frame structure; R4D's extra is gated on lock (8 pcEnd pulses) AND on the structural window, i.e. exactly the guard band where task 11 section 5 measured a skipped valid to disturb NOTHING, and the >= 24 trigger means the emitted entry is genuine buffered data, never empty-ring garbage. Injector tests 134 green (123 pre-existing + 11 new TestR4D), including the two-edge disjointness, the registered FULL-side decision, one-extra-per-window, all three lineages, symmetric mutual exclusion against R3/R3S/R4/R4B in BOTH directions, and W1+R4D on a kit-shaped tree with verify_zip green for both variants. The witness is now TWO read words (0x234 = {locked, skips[15:0], window_opens[14:0]} as in R4B; 0x238 = {16'b0, extras[15:0]}), W1's eight untouched and the only rewritten W1 line still the single assign data_read. verilator --lint-only ERROR-FREE on all FOUR trees (R4D alone and W1+R4D, both lineages; 57/71/61/75 warnings, ZERO on any r4d_ net). ONE SELF-INFLICTED FALSE ALARM, RECORDED NOT BURIED: the first F3-alone lint reported 7 errors ('cannot find r4d_skips in dotted variable'); the cause was MY sed -- a non-global substitution left the lint call pointing at task 12b's R4B tree while mk built the R4D one, so it linted R4B sources against the R4D wrapper. Re-run against the correct tree: 0 errors. Harness: NEW wrap_byte_sro4d.v + build_sro_rxfix4d.sh (obj dir obj_byte_sro_rxfix4d); FIVE files now declare module wrap_byte_sro so the build greps the verilate log for all four others; task 7's sim_sro.cpp UNMODIFIED and task 12b's wrap_byte_sro4b.v untouched. The wrapper trace now carries a KIND column (0 = skip, 1 = extra) so one histogram covers both edges, and r3Extras is a COUNT again for the first time since task 7, so <p>_ep.txt kind 5 timestamps every extra exactly as task 7 defined it. SMOKE PASSED on 25 frames of n_p000: WRAP4D_FILE/WRAP4D_DEFINE present, 21 packets, rhPE=34, pushes-pops=9 -- IDENTICAL to R4B's smoke, i.e. zero extras at 0 ppm as predicted. SEVEN LEGS RUNNING (r4d_p000/m10/m40/tm10/m10c/p10/lol); NO baseline is re-run -- b_p000/b_m10/b_m40/tb_m10 are task 7's, t_lol task 6's, b_m10c/b_p10 task 12b's, and R4B's own delivered streams are banked for the J6 byte-identity rows. NO BOARD CONTACT; no 146 flash without the operator.
Task 14 CORRECTION to the line above: my claim of 'ZERO warnings on any r4d_ net' was WRONG for the two W1+R4D trees, and the warning was a REAL BUG, not noise. Verilator reported %Warning-WIDTHTRUNC at Frequency_and_Time_Synchronizer.v:165 -- "Output port connection 'r4dWit' expects 32 bits on the pin connection, but pin connection's VARREF 'r4d_wit' generates 64 bits". Cause: R4D widened the witness bus from R4B's one 32-bit read word to TWO (0x234 skips/opens, 0x238 extras), and my width edit updated the Rate_Handle declaration, the pass-through helper and the IP-side ports but MISSED the Symbol_Synchronizer pass-through, whose declaration string carries a trailing comment and so did not match the replacement. Effect if shipped: the ninth/tenth word would be truncated at the Symbol_Synchronizer boundary and the extras count -- the witness for the whole FULL-side mechanism -- would read as zero on silicon. IT IS INVISIBLE IN SIM, because the harness taps r4d_extras hierarchically inside Rate_Handle and never crosses that port; only the lint caught it, and only on the COMBINED trees, because with W1 absent the witness chain is not built at all. FIXED (Symbol_Synchronizer now declares output [63:0] r4dWit) and PINNED by new test 135, which asserts the bus is 64 bits at every one of the six levels and that no [31:0] r4dWit survives anywhere; tests now 135 green. Lint re-run on all four trees: 0 errors, 57/71/59/73 warnings, and the ONLY r4dWit mention left is a benign PINMISSING on the wrapper's TxRxComposite instantiation -- the sim wrapper deliberately does not connect the witness bus, exactly as it does not connect W1's w1Bus. The seven gate legs were NOT affected and keep running: they were launched from the R4D-alone tree, where the witness chain is not built, and every gate quantity is tapped inside Rate_Handle.

Task 14: J4 FIRED -- RXFIX_R4D IS REFUTED, AND THE ASYMMETRY HAS A LOCATION [sim]. The extra pop does exactly what it was designed to do AT THE RING and destroys the receiver two stages downstream. AT THE RING IT IS A CLEAN SUCCESS: on n_p10 push_on_full goes 21 (baseline) -> 28 (R4B) -> ZERO, the ring parks at 22/23 for 178 of 248 frames, and 37 extras fire at frames 130,138,146...422 -- spacing 8, i.e. the predicted one per 8.1 frames; on n_m10 seven extras at frames 11-17 ratchet occupancy 31 -> 24, one per frame, every one at window slot 2. AT THE SYSTEM LEVEL IT IS CATASTROPHIC: framing collapses within three air frames of the FIRST extra on both legs where extras fire. r4d_m10 first extra frame 11, last framed frame 13, t7_ok = 8 of 424 = 98.1 % LOSS (R4B 0.47 %); r4d_p10 first extra frame 130, last framed frame 132, t7_ok = 126 of 424 = 70.3 % LOSS (baseline 4.95 %, R4B 6.60 %). THE LOSSES ARE pdPof-ALIGNED, EXACTLY AS THE COORDINATOR PREDICTED: pd_push_on_full fires at air frames 11 and 13 on n_m10 and at frame 130 on n_p10 -- the same frames as the first extras -- and is ZERO on every other leg of this gate and every leg of task 12b. MECHANISM, AND IT WAS ALREADY WRITTEN DOWN IN THE INJECTOR'S OWN R1 HEADER, FOR THE OTHER DIRECTION: the Preamble_Detector realignment FIFO pops on Delay10_reg[49331], the symbol valid delayed 49,332 enb TICKS, which equals 12,333 VALIDS only while the valid density is exactly 1-in-4; the FIFO therefore sits AT its 12,333 full threshold in steady state, so one extra valid is one extra PUSH INTO A FULL FIFO -> push_on_full -> the symbol is DELETED; Peak_Search counts valids on the undelayed chain and Timing_Adjust on the delayed chain while comparing against Peak_Search's timingOffset, so a deletion on the delayed chain moves the phase between the two epoch spaces PERMANENTLY and framing never recovers. The -1 direction does NOT delete -- the FIFO simply runs one entry short and the tick-scheduled pop still fires -- and pd_pop_on_empty is 0 on every leg of this gate and of task 12b. THAT IS THE WHOLE ASYMMETRY BETWEEN -1 AND +1, AND IT LIVES IN ONE FIFO, NOT IN THE DEFRAMER. GATE: J2, J3, J5, J6, J7, J9, J10, J11 PASS; J1 and J8 FAIL; J4 FIRED. J6 IS THE PRE-REGISTRATION'S STRUCTURAL PREDICTION CONFIRMED EXACTLY: section 1 measured from banked data that the occ >= 24 predicate can never be true after lock on five of seven legs (max oMax = 10 on each), and those five came back BYTE-IDENTICAL to R4B in BOTH _deliv.txt and _seq.txt (cmp clean, 424/424/164/425/414 lines) with zero extras. Skips and extras agree THREE WAYS on every leg (trace lines by kind, r3_skips/r3_extras in _res.txt, kind-4/kind-5 ep records). A SCORING TRAP IS NAMED IN THE REPORT: score_t7.py REFUSES to score the two broken legs ('SEQ RANGE IMPLAUSIBLE') and a naive bounded rescore reports LOSS = 0.00 % on both, because once framing collapses the delivered frames carry no TGEN magic and vanish from the seq-keyed denominator instead of counting as losses; the honest denominator is the 424 air frames FED. CANDIDATE (b) IS NOT CUT and the evidence says it would not help: the damage is a deletion in the PD realignment FIFO, while (b) drops an entry from the Rate_Handle ring two stages upstream -- it changes which ring is short, not the 1-in-4 valid density the PD FIFO's tick-indexed pop depends on. NO BOARD CONTACT.
Task 14: THE enSlack EXPERIMENT IS CORRECTLY AIMED BUT IS NOT RUNNABLE ON THE GATE'S LINEAGE -- PRE-REGISTERED, NOT LAUNCHED [sim]. enSlack (fixctl bit 3, register 0x208) makes the PD FIFO tolerate one extra entry instead of dropping a push, which is aimed exactly at the deletion measured above, so the coordinator's diagnosis is right. BLOCKER: the lineage this entire gate runs on, s1_rtl, HAS NO SLACK PATH AND NO fixctl PORT AT ALL -- s1_rtl Validate_Input_Push_Pop.v:131 is 'assign push_on_full_FIFO = Logical_Operator5_out1 & Compare_To_Constant1_y;' against F3's :136 'assign push_on_full_FIFO = Logical_Operator5_out1 & Compare_To_Constant1_y & ( ~ enSlack);'. enSlack appears in 0 files of s1_rtl and in 6 of s1_rtl_txfix_F3 (FIFO.v, FixCtlDec.v, Preamble_Detector.v, Frequency_and_Time_Synchronizer.v, Validate_Input_Push_Pop.v, QPSK_Rx.v), and F3's TxRxComposite carries eight ports s1_rtl lacks (fixctl, ddrcap_*, beatfix_*). So 'drive fixctl[3]=1 in the wrapper' cannot be done on the tree in use. The experiment IS runnable but it is NOT the two <= 70-min legs budgeted: it needs (1) a NEW wrapper driving .fixctl(32'h8), since the current one is shared with the seven s1_rtl legs and must not move; (2) the FIRST-EVER F3 sim build -- F3 has only ever been linted, never simulated, and its eight extra top-level ports need tie-offs; (3) its OWN control, an F3+R4D leg with fixctl[3]=0, because NO F3 BASELINE EXISTS and every baseline in this gate is s1_rtl, so an F3 number is not comparable to any of them; hence (4) FOUR legs plus a build, not two legs. NOT LAUNCHED: substituting a different lineage and a doubled leg count for the authorised experiment without saying so is the same silent switch that produced the withdrawn R4C proposal. Section 4 of task-14-report.md pre-registers it and it is ready to launch on a word. RECOMMENDATION UNCHANGED FROM TASK 12b: R4B forward-only remains the only shippable steering (the controller's 148-only GO already assumes it), and R4D must NOT be flashed to 146 -- it is far worse than doing nothing.
