Task 21: PRE-REGISTRATION COMMITTED BEFORE ANY R4E CODE [sim]. two_jup/comb/RXFIX_R4E_SIM_GATE.md -- no injector edit, no wrapper, no build, no leg exists yet. THE DROP-SCHEDULING FORMULA IS DERIVED FROM THE POINTER ARITHMETIC AND THE BRIEF'S SKETCH IS CORRECTED: Push_Counter/Pop_Counter advance only on validated events and occupancy never exceeds 32, so valid push index p is popped as valid pop index p; Delay_out1 is registered from MATLAB_Function_block2's COMBINATIONAL next state, so O(t) = P(<t) - Q(<t) exactly; a push dropped at tick t would have been emitted on the (O(t)+1)-th validated pop at ticks >= t; hence with m = validated pops in [t,T] (T = the pcEnd tick), THE VANISHED SYMBOL LANDS IN WINDOW SLOT k = O(t) + 1 - m, and in-window requires O-12 <= m <= O. COROLLARY, AND IT IS WHY A PERIOD REGISTER EXISTS: at pcEnd itself slot k's symbol was pushed (O - k) pushes BACK -- not (O - 1 + k) as the brief sketched -- i.e. 11 to 23 pushes IN THE PAST for O >= 24, so the decision cannot be taken at pcEnd and must be taken 12-31 validated pops BEFORE a pcEnd that has not happened yet. No structural signal sits there (pcStart is ~12,320 pops away), so the predictor is a MEASURED period: r4e_period = validated pops between the last two pcEnds, r4e_pcnt = validated pops since the last one, k_hat = O + 1 - period + pcnt, arm band k_hat in [7,11] ASYMMETRIC ON PURPOSE because every staleness source (2 registered ticks, the wait for a strobe, a push in the gap) pushes k UP and never down. TASK 14 SECTION 3 PREDICTS THIS VARIANT FAILS AND IS CONFRONTED IN SECTION 0.1: its claim that any +1/-1 at Rate_Handle propagates into the 1-in-4 valid density is FALSE FOR A PUSH DROP -- the pop is a rigid mod-4 beat and valid_pop = pop & ~pop_on_empty, so at occupancy >= 24 every pop that would have fired still fires and validOut is bit-for-bit the same beats; only WHICH symbol rides each beat changes, which is strictly LESS downstream footprint than R4B's skip (density -1, and pdPof = 0 on all 14 banked legs). K5 (pdPof 0, pdOcc flat 12333, every leg) is the row that settles it and refutes R4E if it fails. TWO GATED PRECONDITIONS BEFORE THE SWEEP, both pre-registered: (A) the smoke leg is n_m10 and NOT n_p000, because at 0 ppm the ring never reaches 24 after lock so a p000 smoke exercises ZERO drops, while n_m10 sits at oMax 31 from lock (frame 11) through frame 73; (B) the pcEnd-to-pcEnd interval in VALIDATED POPS has never been measured -- the wrapper emits it per pcEnd and the smoke does not pass unless its post-lock spread is <= 6, because a wider spread walks the landing slot out of [1,13] for reasons unrelated to the mechanism. A DECLARED RE-CENTRING BRANCH is written down before any histogram is read: any smoke landing slot >= 13 re-centres the target to k_hat in [5,9] and re-smokes. Banked-data prediction table recomputed from the Task 12b _frames.txt: five of seven legs have max oMax = 10 after lock and CANNOT differ from R4B; n_m10 has 63 frames at oMax >= 24 (11-73) and n_p10 has 300 (129-428). T7_NAIR=428 is recorded as a SILENT-PASS HAZARD, not a setting: unset, score_t7.py's SEQ RANGE IMPLAUSIBLE refusal never runs. NO BOARD CONTACT.

Task 21: R4E CUT, BUILT, LINTED AND UNIT-TESTED; SMOKE RUNNING [sim]. Commits 43cfaf1 (pre-registration, before any code), 10b27b7 (the variant + wrap_byte_sro4e.v + build_sro_rxfix4e.sh), 65904ed (tests, scorers, leg launcher, W1_REGMAP section 6). THE RTL: R4E is a CLONE of R4B (skip side character for character after the prefix rename, pinned by test 138 on code lines) plus one FULL-side edit -- FIFO_block is instantiated with .push(strobe & ~r4e_drop_en) instead of .push(strobe), which is a DROPPED PUSH, the natural push_on_full event's own shape. NO POP TERM IS ADDED: Logical_Operator_out1 is R4B's line verbatim (test 139 also bans r4e_do_extra/r4e_extra_en/r4e_extras/phase2 outright), so validOut density is unchanged and the Preamble_Detector realignment FIFO -- the stage that killed R4D -- sees nothing. THE SCHEDULE IS A MEASURED PERIOD: r4e_pcnt counts VALIDATED pops (FIFO_validPop) since the last pcEnd, r4e_period latches the interval, and the registered arm band is pcnt + occ in [period+6, period+10], i.e. k_hat in [7,11] -- ASYMMETRIC because every staleness source (2 registered ticks, the wait for a strobe, a push in the gap) pushes the realised k UP and never down. THE LANDING SLOT IS MEASURED, NOT PREDICTED: at the drop the RTL latches pcnt and occupancy; at the next pcEnd it computes k = occ + 1 - (pcnt - land_a) and updates {land_last, land_min, land_max, land_out} in the TENTH W1 read word 0x238 alongside r4e_drops[15:0] (0x234 keeps R4B's layout byte for byte). The brief asked for a 13-bin histogram in one word; it does not fit beside a 16-bit counter, so the silicon form is the ORDER STATISTIC and the deviation is stated in W1_REGMAP.md section 6.1, with the full histogram produced in sim by the wrapper. K12 LINT PASSES ON ALL FOUR TREES: 0 errors and 0 warnings on any r4e_ net for R4E alone and W1+R4E on both s1_rtl and txfixF3; warning counts 57/71/59/73 are IDENTICAL to R4B's four logs. TESTS: 151 green (135 pre-existing unchanged + 16 new TestR4E, 136-151), including the RXFIX_R4/R4B-prefix hazard closed in both directions, W1+R4E on a kit-shaped tree with verify_zip green on both zips, and the 64-bit witness bus pinned at every hierarchy level. HARNESS: wrap_byte_sro4e.v is a SIXTH file declaring module wrap_byte_sro and prints WRAP4E_FILE/WRAP4E_DEFINE; build_sro_rxfix4e.sh greps the verilate log for the other five and refuses a tree carrying any other steering marker; sim_sro.cpp links UNMODIFIED. The wrapper's landing recount is INDEPENDENT of the steering: occupancy from (fifoPush - fifoPop) mod 32 (NOT occTrue/Delay_out1, the net r4eOcc carries), m from fifoVPop, reference from pcE. It also writes <p>_period.txt, one line per pcEnd carrying the interval in VALIDATED POPS -- the precondition the whole schedule rests on and which had never been measured; the smoke does not pass unless the post-lock spread is <= 6. SMOKE IS n_m10 AND NOT n_p000, because at 0 ppm the ring never reaches 24 after lock so a p000 smoke would exercise ZERO drops, while n_m10 sits at oMax 31 from lock (frame 11) through frame 73. Task 16: THE REVERSE LEG IS MEASURED FOR THE FIRST TIME, IT IS CREDITED, AND ALL THREE PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTIONS ARE FALSIFIED [silicon]. Leg 2 (unit rev-before2, run two_jup/comb/runs/20260905_064400_legB_rev_before2, 06:44:00-06:59:34, the ONE re-run the brief allows). CREDIT: live window 718 s of 746 s (>= the 300 s bar), deliver_rate_pre=995 and deliver_rate_post=995 (>= 900 f/s both), watchdog_relaunch_rx=0 watchdog_relaunch_peer=0 -> CREDITED, labelled WEDGE-TRUNCATED. python3 two_jup/accept_analyze.py <cap>/frames.bin: "live 718s/746s [WEDGE truncated] PER=9.644% (84437/875535) CP95UL=9.706% lag33=-0.030 bins={'1': 35636, '2': 18028, '3-4': 3075, '5-20': 304, '21-100': 2, '>100': 0}"; POOLED PER=9.644% (84437/875535) CP95UL=9.706%; GATE (<1% at CP95 UL) NOT MET. n=875,535 transmitted slots with lost frames IN the denominator. THE PREDICTIONS: PER predicted 3.0-4.5 %, measured 9.644 % -> FALSIFIED, more than 2x worse. Lag family predicted lag-32, measured a clean lag-25 HARMONIC FAMILY -- comb_autocorr all-loss top8 = 25:+0.4443, 50:+0.4065, 125:+0.3751, 75:+0.3690, 63:+0.3666, 100:+0.3312 against a shuffle floor of 0.0037, with lag32=+0.1192 (less than a third of the 25 line) and lag33=-0.0099 (at zero) -> FALSIFIED. Period predicted 25.3 +/- 0.6 ms, measured BEST P=25.39003 frames = 20.3864 ms R=0.2318; a 25.2971 ms line does fall inside the predicted window but at R=0.0817, COMB_LINE=reduced, 0.470 of the a1r2 baseline -> FALSIFIED as the dominant line. UNIT TRAP, flagged so nobody conflates them: the reverse BEST line is 25.39003 FRAMES = 20.3864 ms, while the forward comb was 25.3872 MS -- similar-looking numbers, different quantities. And R at exactly P=32.000 is 0.0010: a deterministic mod-32 beat is ABSENT on the reverse leg.
Task 16: DP1 -- THE ">6 %" BRANCH FIRES, BUT ITS LABEL IS MISLEADING AND THAT IS PART OF THE RESULT [silicon]. Pre-registered verbatim: "credited PER < 1 % -> 'no FULL comb on this pair'; > 6 % or > 100-frame bursts -> 'wedge class dominates'". PER 9.644 % > 6 %, so the "wedge class dominates" branch is the one that fired and is reported as such. The evidence does NOT support the mechanism that label implies: "wedge dominates" predicts long delivery outages inside the scored window and the scored window has NONE -- zero loss runs > 100, only 2 runs in 21-100, and 94 % of the 57,045 loss onsets are singles (35,636) or doubles (18,028). comb_census over the live window is MAGIC-dominated (fail_class n=875,012: OK 791,106, MAGIC 68,509, LEN 1,825, CRC 13,572, ZEROTAIL 0). So the credited 9.644 % is a FINE-GRAINED SINGLES/DOUBLES COMB AT HIGH RATE -- the same KIND of defect the forward leg had before R4B (8.309 %, singles/doubles) but on a DIFFERENT period and lag family. The wedge is real and truncated the leg at 718 s; it is not what produced the 9.644 %.
Task 16: AN ANALYSIS ERROR I MADE AND CORRECTED, kept auditable because the wrong answer was consequential. My first comb_census run passed --txlog <cap>/txlog.bin. On LEG=B the RX board is 146 and THE TRANSMITTER IS 148 = THE PEER, so the TX<->RX join must use txlog_peer.bin. The wrong log returned lost_rx=84437 never_sent=84437 sent_not_decoded=0, which reads as TX-side starvation and would have been a false and load-bearing finding. Re-run with --txlog <cap>/txlog_peer.bin (n_records=979,844): lost_rx=84437 never_sent=0 sent_not_decoded=84437. EVERY ONE of the 84,437 lost frames was transmitted by 148 and not decoded by 146 -- the loss is genuinely RECEIVE-SIDE on 146. Both outputs are kept (comb_census.txt, comb_census_peertx.txt).
Task 16: CONSEQUENCE FOR THE CAMPAIGN -- THE REVERSE DEFECT IS NOT THE FORWARD DEFECT. Forward (before R4B) was lag-32 at 25.39 ms; reverse is a lag-25 family at 20.39 ms with NO mod-32 line at all (R@32.000 = 0.0010). A fix reasoned from the forward comb's geometry (R4B / R4D / R4E) must NOT be assumed to transfer to 146. This STRENGTHENS the case for T18/T19 rather than weakening it: the W1 witness on 146 is now the only way to tell which ring edge, if any, produces a 25-family comb, and the T19 branch table is unchanged and untested.
Task 16: WEDGE-CLASS LEAD, held as a lead and NOT promoted to a mechanism [silicon, lead]. Carrier-reset counter rstcs (register 0x150, capture_r3.sh:66): leg 1 (wedged at 12 s) ran 0x79C -> 0x1273 in 3.36 s (~2,263 resets, ~670/s) BEFORE its window opened, CAP_START 0x174C, post 0x5644 = ~17,361 resets in ~20 s; leg 2 (ran 718 s) read 0x0 pre-window and at CAP_START, post 0x48A = ~1,162 resets in ~735 s (~1.6/s). frame_taxonomy on leg 1 scores cfo_dither cfc_dev_ratio_err_vs_ok = 44.973 (conf HIGH) and carrier_reset_storm phi = 0.1179; Task 13's credited FORWARD leg on the same pair had no carrier resets. The leg that wedged instantly was already storming before its window opened; the leg that survived started calm. WHY THIS IS ONLY A LEAD: leg 2 reads rstcs=0x0 at CAP_START where leg 1 read 0x174C, and I have NOT established whether rstcs is arm-cleared -- bringup_r2r3.sh:108 says only that a false FTS state "0x110 cannot clear", which is a different claim. Until that is settled the two legs' counters may not share an origin. NEXT INSTRUMENT (cheap): read 0x150 before and after an arm on a quiet board. Separately, the IQ tap was DEGENERATE on BOTH legs (occupied BW 42.04 / 41.81 MHz vs ~23 expected), so NO IQ-based conclusion is drawn; frames.bin and the host counters are unaffected and are what every number above uses.
Task 16: CORRECTION TO MY OWN PRE-REGISTRATION, AND IT ADMITS A LEG RATHER THAN EXCLUDING ONE. RXFIX_NIGHT2_PREREG.md section 1 (written 06:40, before any leg) also required capture_r3_exit=0 and no WEDGE verdict. That condition is NOT in the governing documents -- I imported it from legrun_go.sh's composite deliver_rate_gate_pass, which is a leg-completed-clean gate, not the credit rule. The governing rule appears three times identically: brief "credit needs >= 300 s live AND the rate gate"; plan "live window >= 300 s AND deliver-rate gate; wedge-truncated windows scored by accept_analyze.py and labelled"; brief T16 "a wedge-truncated >= 300 s window is still scored and labelled". My condition made that last clause UNSATISFIABLE -- every wedge-truncated window sets capture_r3_exit=3 and writes a WEDGE verdict, as both legs did. Struck in a dated section 1.1 with section 1 left standing as committed. Stated plainly because the correction runs in the direction that deserves MORE scrutiny: without it tonight yields no reverse number at all; with it, leg 2 is credited on 718 s of live link and a rate gate that passed on its own terms. It changes WHICH RULE IS APPLIED, not any measured value. Also pinned: legrun's wedge_verdict onset (508 s) and accept_analyze's live window (718 s) are DIFFERENT COMPUTATIONS and the credit rule names accept_analyze's -- leg 1 shows the divergence the other way (verdict 12 s vs live window 0 s).

Task 17: INSTRUMENT WORK DONE AT THE DESK, ZERO BOARD CONTACT, TESTS GREEN. two_jup/rxfix/w1leg_go.sh gains three knobs: LEG=A|B, BOARD=148|146, RSSI=0|1 (defaults LEG=A BOARD=148 RSSI=0 reproduce Task 10/13 exactly). (1) LEG/BOARD CONSISTENCY IS A REFUSAL, NOT A WARNING: LEG=A is 146 TX -> 148 RX and LEG=B is 148 TX -> 146 RX, and the script READS the RX board, so LEG=B BOARD=148 (or LEG=A BOARD=146) would sweep the ring witness on the TRANSMITTING board and silently score the wrong side of the link -- it now exits 2 with W1LEG_REFUSED (verified: exit code 2). (2) The board is parameterised end to end: brd(), w1_read.sh BOARD=, seqbist_read.py's argument and legrun_go.sh's LEG= all follow BOARD/LEG instead of the old hardcoded 148/A. (3) RSSI=1 adds a THIRD ssh round trip per reading, strictly AFTER the W1 sweep and the checker read, never concurrent -- the W1/checker reads share the single direct_reg_access address-select register and an interleave would silently return another reader's address. THE DEVICE IS LOCATED BY CAPABILITY AT RUNTIME AND FAILS LOUDLY: the remote snippet walks /sys/bus/iio/devices/iio:device* for the first device exposing in_voltage0_rssi and prints RSSI_DEV_NOT_FOUND (exit 9) if none does -- iio:deviceN numbering is not stable across kernels/boots, so it is never hardcoded (a probe on 148 found rssi/hardwaregain/decimated_power all on adrv9002-phy, which happens to be iio:device2 today; that index is deliberately NOT baked in). (4) PERIOD DISCIPLINE: the reading period must stay 10.0 s, so if the three serial round trips overrun 12 s the RSSI read drops to EVERY SECOND reading rather than stretching the cadence -- the W1 deltas are what the period protects. (5) The AIR path adds 0x150 (rstcs) to the AUX sweep -- swept in the SAME ssh round trip as the W1 words, so it costs no extra trip -- giving a per-reading carrier-reset timeline (PREREG sec 5.2). MODE=ctrl deliberately KEEPS the original AUX "0x104 0x124" so its control table stays byte-comparable with Task 13's, and a test pins that. TESTS: two_jup/tests/test_rxfix_rig_scripts.py grew 9 tests (45 passed, was 36), all DRY with the ssh/scp shim; an empty shim log is the proof of zero board contact, and every new test asserts it.
Task 19: PRE-REGISTRATION ADDITION COMMITTED BEFORE THE LEG (RXFIX_NIGHT2_PREREG.md sec 5.1/5.2), per the controller's 07:28 ruling. 5.1 THE RATE-VS-PERIOD COMPARISON: T16 changed what T19 must answer -- the reverse comb is a lag-25 family at 20.3864 ms, not the forward lag-32 / 25.39 ms, so "an edge counter advanced" is no longer enough; the edge must advance AT THE COMB'S OWN RATE. Procedure: from the 10-s W1 readings on 146 take whichever of push_on_full or pop_on_empty (word 0x218) actually advances, form WRAP-AWARE deltas between consecutive readings, convert to a mean interval, and compare against THAT SAME LEG'S OWN PER comb period from comb_period_ms.py (not T16's number, not the forward leg's). Equal within 2 % => the ring edge IS the event behind the comb => branch W or b by which counter moved. Differing by > 2 % => TWO DISTINCT PROCESSES => branch (c) with both raw numbers; the worked example is stated in advance so it cannot be reinterpreted afterwards -- the ring at ~25.3 ms while the losses sit at ~20.4 ms means two processes. Neither counter advancing = branch (a), no interval computed. Both intervals quoted with sample counts (number of 10-s readings, number of edge events). 5.2 rstcs BECOMES A STANDING MEASUREMENT: record register 0x150 before, at and after the window on 146 for every leg, with the elapsed time between them. On a legrun leg capture_r3.sh already emits it (regs_pre/regs_cap/regs_post) so it is a reporting duty; on a w1leg air leg the reader now captures it via the AUX sweep. This also settles the question that currently blocks the T16 carrier-storm lead from being promoted to a mechanism -- with 0x150 read on both sides of a known arm, whether the counter is ARM-CLEARED becomes directly observable.

Task 23: CLASS NAMED [desk, banked data, no board contact]. The forward residual 0.224 % is NOT the prereg's delivery/stall class. It is a BYTE-ALIGNMENT CASCADE in the receive delivery path, triggered by a single decode error at the decoder pins: one CRC-fail costs the host 8.59 frames instead of 1 because the following ~6-15 frames arrive DISPLACED BY A FIXED 568 BYTES inside the carve slice until the stream resyncs. Measured on 159 bursts: position 0 = 157/159 CRC-fail; position 1 = 159/159 MAGIC with 151 carrying no frame magic at all; positions >=3 = 96-97 % with magic_off == 568. Over the live window 1,305 of 1,543 class-1 failures (84.6 %) carry the frame magic and 1,259 of those sit at exactly offset 568, against README_hostlog.md sec 3.3's 2.3 % chance floor -- the "large excess would be a genuinely new observation" that file asks for. The W1 baseline's comb failures do NOT look like this (18.5 % carry a magic, dominant offset 64). Q1 CLUSTERED (CV 2.473, Fano 3.2-4.3, chi2 vs uniform p=3.9e-18, KS vs exponential p=4.5e-26) with ONE coherent 8.140 s rate line (R=0.5499 on 50 cluster onsets, family-wise p<2.5e-4 uniform / p=0.0003 clustering-preserving; split-half coherent in three RX subsets AND the independent failhdr ring; survives the reader-off control at identical event rate 0.3039 vs 0.3045 /s; already present in the W1 baseline's 5-20 class and absent from its singles/3-4) -- so the prereg's "no single period" FAILS. Q2 MAGIC 88.8 %, ZEROTAIL 0 and class4 fzo==0 count 0 with RXQ_ZEROHDR confirmed OFF (so an unfilled carve slot would land there by construction) => ZERO RX delivery holes; NEVER_SENT 0/1956 => zero TX starvation on 146. Q3 on the exact 46-interval common window (20:20:44-20:28:24, 460.0 s, denominators 0.9993): checker 309 lost slots / 0.0540 % vs host 1,229 / 0.2145 %, but 144 vs 143 EVENTS (Pearson r=+0.935, p=1.8e-21) and CRC 129 vs 128 (1.01x) while garbage 164 vs 956 (5.83x) -- the events are born at or upstream of the pins one-for-one and the delivery path multiplies their cost 4x; slot budget 293 at the pins / 791 created downstream / 145 no-record. Q4 the five 4-s flatlines and the six ~10 % census dips are BOTH instrument artifacts (min 4-s clean-frame sum 4,883 vs a <200 threshold, 0 flatline seconds, daemon dma_rx_ok has 0 steady-state zero-deltas -- a 4 s poll against a 5.00 s log cadence; the dips scale d_frames 0.9019 and d_r4b_skips 0.9015 together, ratio-of-ratios 0.9995, and 3 of 6 contain zero host loss). Q5 UNANSWERED: 0 of 3 candidate T17 legs carry board data (all dry=1 rehearsals), no rssi column anywhere; substitute RF evidence rstcs=0 on all 871,598 records, CFO shift 0.008 sd, biterr +1.4 %. Baseline: the 5-20 class rate fell 0.3129 -> 0.2300 /s (-27 %), PER contribution 0.2787 -> 0.2092 pp -- pre-existing, not new. Tool: accept_analyze.py --burst-times (default path byte-identical, 7 new tests pass, pinned to comb/common.py's interpolation). Next instrument proposed: a per-event ring in rx_seq_checker read over the W1/R4B register path, joined to the host failhdr ring by frame index. Deliverable two_jup/comb/FWD_RESIDUAL_0p22.md; report two_jup/sdd_archive/2026-09-04-rxfix/task-23-report.md.

Task 26: FIX IMPLEMENTED + PRE-REGISTERED [desk, no board contact]. GEOMETRY, computed from the parser: the RX drain carves the completed DMA area at a FIXED pkt_bytes=1528 stride (rx_pump_queued; the 3080 B air frame and SLOT_BYTES=2048 are TX-side and irrelevant here), and the ONLY re-anchor in the path is the next DMA transfer, whose request is hard-gated on the frame-sync tuser. A whole-frame drop is therefore harmless (1528 B = a whole slot, alignment preserved, cost 1 slot); a NON-multiple anomaly Delta puts every following frame at a CONSTANT phase (-Delta) mod 1528. The measured 568 inverts to Delta = -960 B (120 words) or +568 B (71 words) -- the sign is exactly what FWD_RESIDUAL_0p22.md's proposed checker event ring would settle; 376 -> -1152 B (144 w) and 1136 = 2x568 (two events in one transfer). Every measured offset is a whole 64-bit word. CASCADE BOUNDARY IDENTIFIED (Task 23 left it open): it is the RX DMA transfer at -M 16 (bringup_r2r3.sh:171 RXM_EFF=${RXM:-16}; the judged leg's meta.txt has rxm_148= unset), so run length = 17 - j and the ceiling is rx_multi+1 = 17 -- exactly the longest run measured; mean 1956/213 = 9.183 against 9.0 for uniform position-in-transfer (residual: length-2 is 1 observed vs ~12 expected, unexplained). CRUX: at phase 568 the displaced frame is SPLIT ACROSS TWO SLOTS (960 B in one, 568 B in the next), so a scan confined to one slice finds the magic and can NEVER validate the CRC -- it would count re-anchors and recover nothing, firing the brief's falsifier for the wrong reason. The re-anchor must be a BYTE CURSOR into the contiguous carve. FIX: pure qpsk_frame_resync() (qpsk_frame.c:181) scans 8-byte steps, magic pre-filter then full qpsk_frame_decode, bounded to max_shift < pkt_bytes so a hit at exactly +pkt_bytes (the ordinary next slot) can never be miscounted as a re-anchor; rx_dphase byte cursor + rx_resync_try() in the QUEUED drain only (qpsk_tun.c:1667/1080), re-anchor runs AFTER st.crc_drops++/framelog_record_fail so the fail-class taxonomy, magic_off and every offline census are unchanged; no on-disk ABI touched (failhdr pad stays 0). 8-byte step is correctness not tuning: carve_copy_from does volatile 64-bit loads on /dev/mem O_SYNC and an unaligned one FAULTS on ARM64. Legacy multi drain, cyclic ring and the eager path deliberately UNCHANGED (the legacy path is the RXQ=0 FIFO A/B control arm). QPSK_RX_RESYNC=0 restores historical behaviour from the SAME binary => same-build A/B. COUNTERS: new separate line "qpsk_tun rxresync: on/phase/resync_568/resync_other/resync_fail/recovered/tail_lost"; the stats: line is byte-identical and the nakstat=4 / rxqstat=1 strings fingerprints are unchanged. TESTS: new test_rxresync.c, 40 checks 0 failures, driving the DEPLOYED rx_pump_queued() against a fake DMA regfile with -DQPSK_CARVE_2MB; synthetic 960 B deletion goes from 11 lost / 5 delivered (resync OFF) to 1 lost / 15 delivered, resync_568=1, recovered=10, tail_lost=1; the W1 comb (garbage, magic at 64) still classifies MAGIC/magic_off=64, does NOT re-anchor, and gives IDENTICAL accounting ON and OFF (3 lost / 13 delivered, resync_fail=3); clean area is inert; rx_dphase cleared by both rx_q_on_complete and rx_arm_queued. Full suite green (frame 578, k5 89, ber, whiten, seq, txq 25, txlog 25, rxresync 40). INCIDENTAL: test_k5 did NOT BUILD at HEAD (commit 0822e3e turned AXR_HOLE_SZ/AXR_RENAK into runtime vars without updating test_k5.c) -- confirmed pre-existing on a pristine git-archive tree; repaired by a 2-identifier rename in test_k5.c only, committed separately. BINARY (deploy_daemon_go.sh line, NAKKEEP resolved): gcc -O2 -Wall -DQPSK_CARVE_2MB -DQPSK_ARQ_NAKSTAT -DQPSK_RXQ_STAT, nakstat=4 PASS, md5=023d7bfd6db5706e37465ea0ce7bd967 (LOCAL x86-64 build of these sources+flags -- NOT a deploy fingerprint; the board builds aarch64). PRE-REGISTRATION two_jup/comb/RXFIX_HOSTFIX_PREREG.md: PER <= 0.08 %, events unchanged ~0.30/s, resync_568 ~= event count, recovered ~= 5.5x resync_568, checker gap events ~3/10 s and lost slots ~0.054 % and r4b witnesses ~0.0314 ALL UNCHANGED (the control arm); falsifier F1 = resync_568 counting with PER unchanged => displaced frames are corrupted not shifted. FINDING FOR THE CONTROLLER: the brief's "cost per event -> ~1 frame" is optimistic and the derived prediction is THINNER than the registered bound -- 2.05 slots/event are already unparseable at the pins and 1.01 straddles the transfer boundary, so the achievable floor is (293+145)/572901 = 0.0764 % against a 0.08 % bound, ~5 % headroom; at 90 % recovery PER lands at 0.0902 % and P1 fails while the fix works. Bound left at the brief's verbatim 0.08 %; prereg falsifier F2 covers that case as PARTIAL. NO BOARD TOUCHED; deploy + judge leg remain the controller's to schedule. Report two_jup/sdd_archive/2026-09-04-rxfix/task-26-report.md.

Task 27: TWO EXECUTION FACTS FOUND AT THE DESK BEFORE ANY BOARD WRITE, both of which change how this task must be run and reported. (1) capture_r3.sh REBUILDS 148's DAEMON AT THE START OF EVERY LEG from the repo's host_app_k5 sources (capture_r3.sh:106 scp, :128 gcc -O2 -Wall -DQPSK_CARVE_2MB $NAKKEEP ${HOST_CFLAGS:-} $BCF), and BCF=$HOST_CFLAGS_B is scoped to board B/146 ONLY by design ("board A (148) must rebuild to a functionally untouched binary"). So whatever deploy_daemon_go.sh installs on 148 is OVERWRITTEN by capture_r3's rebuild when the judge leg starts -- the binary actually under test is capture_r3's build, not the deploy's, and the report must fingerprint the leg's binary rather than the deploy's. This is benign for the fix itself: capture_r3 scps from host_app_k5 at HEAD, which carries the Task 26 resync sources, and the re-anchor is compiled in UNCONDITIONALLY (only the QPSK_RX_RESYNC env var gates it at runtime), so the judge leg does test the fix. (2) THE COUNTER SPLIT: rxresync_dump() at qpsk_tun.c:1088 is NOT behind any #ifdef and is called unconditionally at :279, so P4/P5/P6/P12 (resync_568 / recovered / resync_other / resync_fail) are readable from ANY build including capture_r3's -- but rxq_stats_dump() IS behind #ifdef QPSK_RXQ_STAT (:275), which capture_r3 strips on 148, so P11 (rx_q_resets) is NOT measurable on a default judge leg. Getting P11 needs HOST_CFLAGS=-DQPSK_RXQ_STAT (HOST_CFLAGS applies to BOTH boards; HOST_CFLAGS_B is 146-only). Current 148 daemon fingerprint before any change: md5 1f834433f7dac41e774e88f9a833f041 (matches Task 10's recorded app 1f834433), nakstat=4, rxqstat=0, rxresync=0 -- i.e. a PLAIN build with no resync code, rebuilt by capture_r3 during the T17 leg at 07:51.

Task 20: S1 (R4D + R1) PASSES EVERY GATE ROW AND THE FALSIFIER DOES NOT FIRE -- THE +10 ppm LEG LOSES NOTHING [sim]. r4dr1_p10: LOSS 0.00 % on BOTH denominators (seq-keyed 421/421 OK, CORRUPT=0, MISSING=0; air-frames-fed t7_ok=424 of 424) against baseline b_p10 4.99/4.95 %, shipped R4B 6.65/6.60 % and task 14's R4D 70.28 % -- WITH ALL 37 EXTRA POPS FIRING at frames 130,138,...,422, spacing mean 8.11 (8:32 9:4), rh_push_on_full 0 (baseline 21, R4B 28), the ring parked at oS/oE 22/22-23/23, pdPof 0 and pdOcc PINNED at 12333. Against all 428 air frames fed including the 4-frame acquisition transient the same leg reads 4/428 = 0.93 % (baseline 5.84, R4B 7.48, R4D 70.56) -- no reading of the denominator changes a verdict. SO THE ANSWER TO THE BRIEF'S QUESTION IS (a): THE R4D COLLAPSE WAS THE PREAMBLE_DETECTOR REALIGNMENT FIFO'S DELETION, NOT THE +1 VALID ITSELF IN THE PEAK_SEARCH/TIMING_ADJUST EPOCH SPACES. R4DR1 EVEN RECOVERS FRAMES THE BASELINE LOSES: all 21 seq b_p10 drops are delivered, and seq 289 and 362, which arrive CORRUPT in both b_p10 and r4b_p10 (nbad 73 and 241 bytes), arrive byte-perfect (ok=1 nbad=0). On r4dr1_m10 the only 2 losses (seq 133,134) align to the SKIPS (best constant offset -8, 2/2 within +/-1, 1 of 37 events) and are the SAME TWO SEQ R4B LOSES -- an inherited R4B residual, not created by the mirror; the 7 extras at frames 11-17 ratchet the ring 31 -> 22/23 and framing is alive through all of them (R4D died at frame 13). GATE: S1 S2 S3 S4 S5 S6 S7 S8 S9 all PASS; S1b and S10 land on the pre-registered branch two and gate nothing. Task 20: THE DECOMPOSITION IS CLEAN AND MEASURED -- R4D OWNS THE RING, R1 OWNS THE FRAMING [sim]. Three independent md5 comparisons across all seven legs: (1) the ring's per-frame state, _frames.txt columns 21-26 (occTS occTE occTmin occTmax rhPopEmpty rhPushFull), is BIT-IDENTICAL to R4D on 7 of 7 legs; (2) every steered decision is identical -- _skipwin.txt (slot_rtl, slot_harness, dt_enb, occ, locked, kind) matches on 7 of 7 and the (kind, air frame) stream of _ep.txt kinds 4/5 matches on 7 of 7, i.e. the same 37 extras at the same frames on p10, the same 7 at 11-17 on m10, the same 8/210/24/60/17 skips; (3) the ONLY _ep.txt column that moves is pdOcc AT THE EVENT: 12332/12333 under R4D, 12333 ALWAYS under R4DR1. R1 is two stages downstream of the ring and changes nothing upstream, so the FULL-edge win is entirely R4D's and framing survival is entirely R1's. WHAT R1 DOES TO THE STREAM IS MOVE 'WHEN', NOT 'WHAT': on every skip-only leg t12_ident PASSES with content equal on ALL common seq (p000 423/423, m10c 422/422, m40 407/407) and the sidx annotation of the frames following a skip is later by exactly +4 input samples = ONE SYMBOL (p000 0:415 +4:8; m10c 0:362 +4:60; m40 0:200 +4:207) -- under R4D the tick-scheduled pop fires into a hole and the frame completes one symbol early, under R1 it completes on its true 12,333rd valid. BOTH PRE-NAMED TRAPS WERE REAL: pdPof=0 is a TAUTOLOGY under R1 (a push while full always carries its own pop, so push_on_full_FIFO is unreachable by construction) and would have been the emptiest row in the table had it been quoted as the fix's confirmation -- the positive witness is the pdOcc pin, 12333..12333 on all seven legs against R4D's 12332..12333 on five; and the p000 byte-identity row DID come back 'differs', which without the pre-registered disjunction would have read as a broken build. One caveat stated in the report: t12b_window.py is R4B-shaped (kind 5 = lock sentinel there), so it labels the extras 'LOCK' and prints *** MISMATCH; its substantive output stands -- 44/44 steered events on m10 and every event on all seven legs inside slots [1,13] on BOTH the RTL counter and the independent recount, same histograms as R4D, and m10c's 'mid-payload' flag is inherited from R4D's bit-identical event positions.
Task 20: WHAT THIS LICENSES AND WHAT IT DOES NOT [sim]. LICENSES the reverse-leg (146) direction in sim: the FULL edge is now steerable AND survivable -- push_on_full 21 -> 0 with ZERO frame loss at +10 ppm, against a shipped R4B that makes that leg WORSE than doing nothing (6.65 % vs the baseline's 4.99 %). R4D+R1 is the FIRST variant that improves the positive-SRO leg, and it costs nothing where the mirror does not fire (R4DR1 = R4B to the digit on n_m10, = R4D on every skip-only leg). DOES NOT LICENSE A FLASH: this is s1_rtl, the SIM lineage; R1 removes a 49,332-flop shift register's only reader and adds a 14-bit comparator in the RECEIVE path, which has never been through synthesis, and the flashed lineage is s1_rtl_txfix_F3, not s1_rtl -- a silicon cut needs the injector's kit path (both zips + verify_zip), a timing-closed build and its own on-air gate. enSlack (task 14 section 4, fixctl bit 3, F3-only) IS NOT WITHDRAWN but is now the SECOND-best option: R1 fixes the same deletion on the lineage that already simulates, with no new wrapper, no first-ever F3 build and no missing baseline, and it makes the deletion STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE rather than merely tolerated. Report two_jup/sdd_archive/2026-09-04-rxfix/task-20-report.md; raw scoring two_jup/comb/sro_sim/t20_score.txt; pre-registration two_jup/comb/RXFIX_S1_SIM_GATE.md (commit 7ed4430, before any code). No board contact, no Vivado, no subagents; rxfix_inject.py and its tests untouched (task 21 owns them); the stagewin dump reserved for the falsifier branch was not built because the falsifier did not fire.

Task 27: F5 FIRES AND IT IS THE REAL FINDING -- resync_568 = 0, resync_other = 171 [silicon]. The pre-registered mechanism is the 568-byte phase (Task 23/26 measured it in 1,259 of 1,305 cases) and the re-anchor was built to fire there. ON SILICON IT FIRED ZERO TIMES in 721 s, while 171 re-anchors landed at OTHER offsets and recovered 1,237 frames -- 7.2 frames per re-anchor, the same SHAPE P5 predicted, at the wrong offset. So P4 FAILS (predicted resync_568 ~= 140, got 0), P6 FAILS INVERTED (resync_other 171 >> resync_568 0), P5 is not evaluable as written (ratio undefined) and P12 is MOOT -- that row exists to separate two burst-head models and neither applies when the 568 re-anchor never fires. PREREG F5's instruction is explicit and is followed here: "The 568 phase is not the dominant displacement on this leg ... RE-OPEN THE CLASS BEFORE CREDITING THE FIX." THE OUTCOME IS NOT IN DOUBT (same-binary A/B, control verified by effect, fabric invariant, 60 % of the residual gone); WHAT IS REFUTED IS THE PHASE. Either the displacement distribution on this leg differs from the banked capture the 568 figure came from, or the scan reaches a valid frame at another offset first. The fix's VALUE does not depend on which, but the CLASS DESCRIPTION does -- no fabric-side conclusion should be drawn from "568" until it is re-measured. Also: resync_fail=9757 against 171 successes means 98 % of scans find nothing, which is both remaining headroom and a hint about where the real phase is.
Task 27: TWO PREDICTIONS THAT FAIL ON THEIR LETTER BUT ARE NOT THE FIX'S FAULT, recorded so nobody reads them as regressions. P2 ("events unchanged ~0.30/s"): the event COUNT nearly doubles (188 -> 383) while TOTAL loss falls 60 %. That is the run-splitting artefact P13 registered IN ADVANCE -- recovering frames INSIDE what used to be one 5-20-slot run turns it into several 1-3-slot runs, so "events" is not conserved across the two builds; the physically meaningful pair is slots/event 9.17 -> 1.81 and total lost 1724 -> 692. P11 ("rx_q_resets = 0"): leg A reads resets=1, which would trip F3's letter -- but the CONTROL arm reads resets=1 too, on the same binary with the re-anchor disabled, so it is an arm-time artefact common to both and NOT caused by the fix; F3 is not fired. P8 (checker lost slots) moved 0.046 % -> 0.036 %, but the checker sits at the decoder pins UPSTREAM of a host-side fix and cannot be moved by it; with N=1 per arm there is no spread estimate, so F4's "more than its run-to-run spread" CANNOT be evaluated rigorously and is called not-fired on the strength of P9 instead (0.13 % apart), which is the far tighter invariant.

Task 21: K8 FIRED -- RXFIX_R4E IS REFUTED, AND THE GUARD-BAND HYPOTHESIS WITH IT [sim]. THE STEERING IS EXACT AND THE HYPOTHESIS IT WAS BUILT TO TEST IS FALSE. On n_p10 all 37 scheduled push drops land at window slot 7 -- dead centre of the 13-slot structural guard -- on the RTL witness AND on a pointer-based recount that shares no net with the steering, 37/37 agreeing, with k = occ + 1 - m holding on 44 of 44 drops across all legs; the ring parks at 22/23, occTrue_end = 23, push_on_full goes 21 (baseline) -> 28 (R4B) -> ZERO, and pdPof and pd_pop_on_empty are 0 with pdOcc flat at 12333 on ALL SEVEN LEGS. AND EVERY ONE OF THOSE 37 DROPS KILLED A FRAME: 37 of 37 drops are aligned to a lost frame at a CONSTANT offset of 2 air frames and NOT ONE of the 47 lost frames is more than +/-1 from a drop (27 drops cost one frame, 10 cost two). LOSS n_p10 = 11.16 % seq-keyed (expect 421, OK 374, missing 47) and 11.92 % air-frames-fed (t7_ok 377 of 428), against the baseline b_p10 4.99 %/403 and R4B 6.65 %/396. n_m10 repeats it: 7 drops at frames 12-18 cost seq 10-17, eight frames R4B does not lose, 2.38 % vs 0.48 % (t7_ok 414 vs 422 of 428). THE LAW THE GATE EXPOSES: LOSS COUNTS RING DELETIONS, NOT THEIR POSITION. The comb rate is one deletion per 8.11 air frames on all three variants (12,333 x 1e-5 = 0.1233 entries/frame); what a variant changes is WHEN THE COMB STARTS, and the frame count follows mechanically -- b_p10 acts at occupancy 32, first event frame 259, (428-259)/8.11 = 20.8 predicted, 21 measured, 21 lost; r4b_p10 acts at 32 with the ring parked +7 by acquisition skips, first event 202, 27.9 predicted, 28 measured, 28 lost; r4e_p10 acts at TWENTY-FOUR plus the same +7 parking, first event 132, 36.5 predicted, 37 measured, 47 lost. The 127-frame advance decomposes as 65 frames for the lower threshold (8/0.1233) plus R4B's measured 57 frames of acquisition parking = 122 predicted vs 127 measured. THIS IS TASK 12b SECTION 6.1's LAW UNCHANGED IN SIGN: where the steering parks the ring sets when the comb starts, not how fast it runs -- and R4E moved the trigger 8 entries lower, so it started the comb earlier and paid for it. THE FAILURE IS NOT TASK 14's MECHANISM AND TASK 14 SECTION 3's STATED REASON IS STILL WRONG: pdPof = 0 on all seven legs, so the Preamble_Detector realignment FIFO -- the stage that destroyed R4D -- is untouched, exactly as the pre-registration section 0.1 argued. THE K8 PER-STAGE DUMP IS DECISIVE AND IT PROVES A NEGATIVE: over 193 enb beats around the first drop against the matched same-phase window one air frame earlier, validated ring PUSHES are 47 vs 49 (the deletion, visible as a hole in the strobe pattern at rel -1) while validated ring pops (48), Rate_Handle.validOut (48), CFC.validOut (48), Preamble_Detector.validOut (48), Correlator.validOut (48), Packet_Controller.validOut (41), push_on_full (0), pop_on_empty (0), Peak_Search psTref (12159->12164) and Timing_Adjust taRef (12170->12175) are ALL BIT-IDENTICAL. The drop is a pure data deletion: it removes a symbol and leaves every valid count, every FIFO edge and every epoch counter in the receive chain identical to a frame in which nothing happened, so no stage can compensate and nothing flags it. The valid-counted-epoch reading -- tref at a CONSTANT pcEnd-relative position (slot_harness 12317 on all 37) walking 12160 -> 12124, exactly -1 per drop on 36 of 36 deltas, while R4E's own seven EMPTY-side skips on the SAME leg sit at a constant tref 12177 -- is consistent with every measurement and is reported as the reading the evidence supports, NOT as a located stage: unlike Task 14 there is no firing witness at the failing stage, only alignment. GATE: ten of thirteen rows pass (K1 K3 K4 K5 K7 K9 K10 K11 K12 K13); K2, K6 and K8 FAIL and K6 is reported FAIL and not softened to a partial even though four of its five clauses pass. NO BOARD CONTACT.
Task 21: R4E LOSES TO TASK 20's S1 (R4D+R1) ON EVERY LEG WHERE THEY DIFFER; S1 REMAINS THE 146 CANDIDATE [sim]. The two variants act on the SAME edge with the SAME trigger and are IDENTICAL AT THE RING on n_p10 -- 37 events, r3_skips 7, rh_push_on_full 0, occTrue_end 23, pd_push_on_full 0 -- and 47 frames apart at the output: S1 delivers t7_ok = 424 of 428 air frames with t7_nomagic = 3, R4E delivers 377 with t7_nomagic = 50. On n_m10 S1 is 422 of 428 and R4E is 414. On the five legs where neither steers the FULL side (p000, m40, tiled, m10c, lol) R4E is BYTE-IDENTICAL to both R4B and S1 in _deliv.txt and _seq.txt (cmp clean, 424/425, 424/425, 164/165, 425/426, 414/415 lines) with zero drops -- the pre-registration's structural prediction from banked oMax data confirmed exactly on all five. THERE IS NO LEG ON WHICH R4E BEATS S1 AND NONE ON WHICH IT EQUALS S1 EXCEPT THE FIVE WHERE IT DOES NOTHING. The S1 figures are Task 20's own leg outputs read from disk (r4dr1_*_res.txt); nothing in this task re-scored or re-ran them. THE REASON IS R1: S1 pairs the FULL-side perturbation with RXFIX_R1, which makes the PD realignment FIFO's pop VALID-INDEXED rather than tick-indexed, so the +1 is absorbed and the data-to-epoch bookkeeping is maintained by construction; R4E avoids the PD FIFO deletion by adding no valid at all, but nothing in it maintains the data-to-epoch phase across a deleted symbol. RECOMMENDATION: DO NOT BUILD R4E -- it is worse than doing nothing on n_p10 (11.16 % vs 4.99 %) and worse than R4B on n_m10 (2.38 % vs 0.48 %); S1 is unaffected by anything here and this gate independently reproduces its ring behaviour. A hypothetical R4E+R1 is NOT CUT and NOT GATED here; it is a coherent next candidate and it is a new variant with a new gate, and it is not needed while S1 passes. TWO PRE-REGISTERED PRECONDITIONS BOTH HELD AND ONE OF THEM IS A NEW MEASUREMENT: the pcEnd-to-pcEnd interval in VALIDATED POPS is 12,333 modally with post-lock spread 2 on n_p10 and 1 on the smoke (ceiling 6, never measured before this task; the three legs with a wide spread each have exactly one MISSED pcEnd -- n_m10's outlier is 24,666 = 2 x 12,333 -- and none of them drops, so per_ok's saturation guard was never the operative term), and the DECLARED RE-CENTRING BRANCH (any smoke landing slot >= 13 -> retarget k_hat to [5,9] and re-smoke) WAS NOT TAKEN because the smoke measured slot 7 on six drops and 8 on one. The n_m10 skip-side prediction made from banked data before the run landed to the frame: skips move 191 -> 134 (predicted ~133) and rise 30 -> 37 (predicted ~36). Tests 151 green (135 pre-existing unmodified + 16 new TestR4E 136-151); lint 0 errors on four trees with 0 warnings on any r4e_ net and warning counts 57/71/59/73 identical to R4B's.
